### [1] [AI-Model Network: Concept, Current State and Future](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27382)
*Li Zhetao,Zeng Xiyu,Wang Jianhui,Xiao Yong,Liu Zhongren,Wu Junru,Lai Junjie,Huang Jijun,Long Saiqin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文借鉴互联网发展思路，提出了AI模型网络（AI-ModelNet）的概念与系统架构，旨在通过建立模型间的连接路径，实现异构大模型的互联互通、能力共享与协同推理，以解决当前大模型训练成本高、部署复杂及异构模型协作困难等关键瓶颈问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大模型发展面临训练成本高昂、部署复杂度高以及异构模型快速扩散等挑战，导致模型间的有效交互与协作成为亟待解决的关键瓶颈。随着人工智能向轻量化、私有化和领域专用化方向发展，迫切需要新的范式来实现异构模型间的互联互通与能力共享。

Method: 借鉴互联网的发展理念，提出AI模型网络（AI-ModelNet）这一新型范式。首先回顾单模型与多模型研究现状，随后构建AI-ModelNet的系统愿景与分层架构，通过建立模型间的连接路径实现模型互联、能力共享和协同推理，并通过原型系统和多样化应用案例验证框架可行性。

Result: 成功构建了AI-ModelNet的分层系统架构，通过原型系统验证了该框架在实现模型互联、能力共享与协同推理方面的可行性，并展示了其在多样化应用场景中的实际效果。

Conclusion: AI-ModelNet为解决异构大模型协作问题提供了新思路和新范式，未来研究将在该框架基础上进一步探索模型网络的关键技术与研究方向，推动大模型生态的协同发展。

Abstract: While the primary function of computers lies in computation and processing, the core value of the Internet is rooted in sharing and collaboration. Computers create the Internet, and the Internet empowers the value of computers. The rapid development of the Internet, cloud computing, and big data is pushing artificial intelligence into the era of large models (LMs). However, the practical application of LMs is currently hindered by high training costs and deployment complexities, driving a shift toward lightweight, private, and domain-specific models. With the rapid proliferation and wide distribution of heterogeneous models, enabling effective interaction and collaboration among them has emerged as a critical bottleneck that urgently needs to be addressed in LM development. Drawing inspiration from the development of the Internet, this paper proposes the concept, vision, and system architecture of world wide AI-model network (AI-ModelNet). It is a novel paradigm that achieves interconnection, capability sharing, and collaborative reasoning by establishing pathways between models. We first briefly review the current state of single-model and multi-model research. Subsequently, the systemic vision and hierarchical architecture of AI-ModelNet are articulated, followed by validation of the framework's feasibility through a prototype system and diverse application cases. Finally, key directions for future research are discussed preliminarily.

</details>


### [2] [When Does Personality Composition Matter for Multi-Agent LLM Teams?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27443)
*Aryan Keluskar,Amrita Bhattacharjee,Huan Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 人格提示对LLM多智能体团队性能的影响取决于任务结构：在编程任务中影响较小，但在开放式合作和谈判任务中会显著降低性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有研究表明人格提示能够改变大型语言模型的沟通方式（如低宜人性产生对抗性语言，高宜人性变得合作），但这些行为变化是否会影响客观任务结果尚未得到充分探索，也缺乏跨多个领域对沟通风格与任务性能关系的系统性研究。

Method: 通过操纵前沿大语言模型的人格特质，在三个任务领域进行系统性实验：结构化编程任务、开放式研究合作和竞争性谈判任务，考察人格组成对多智能体团队性能的影响。

Result: 人格效应关键性地依赖于任务结构。在编程任务中，低宜人性导致显著的沟通变化，但对里程碑完成影响很小。而在开放式合作和谈判任务中，相同的人格操纵会大幅降低性能表现。

Conclusion: 人格操纵在多智能体系统设计中存在局限性，其对团队性能的影响因任务结构而异，这为多智能体系统的设计提供了重要启示，同时也揭示了人格操纵的边界条件。

Abstract: Personality prompting shapes how large language models communicate, yet whether these behavioral shifts affect objective task outcomes remains under-explored. Prior work shows that agents prompted with low agreeableness produce adversarial language, while those prompted with high agreeableness become cooperative, but the relationship between communication style and task performance has not been systematically examined across multiple domains. In this work, we investigate whether personality composition matters for multi-agent team performance by manipulating personality traits across frontier LLMs on three task domains: structured coding, open-ended research collaboration, and competitive bargaining. We find that personality effects depend critically on task structure. In coding tasks, low agreeableness leads to large communication shifts that have little effect on milestone completion. In open-ended collaboration and bargaining, the same manipulation substantially degrades performance. We discuss implications for multi-agent system design and the limits of personality manipulation.

</details>


### [3] [AI-Driven Synthesis for High-Tech System Design: Automating Innovation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28126)
*Luuk Oerlemans,Steven Westerhof,Theo Hofman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为自动化设计(AiD)的变革性范式，利用计算设计合成(CDS)框架结合深度学习和生成式AI，实现新系统的自动化创建，通过两个案例研究证明了该方法能够实现从仿真优化到最小人工监督的自主设计的根本性转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代高科技系统设计面临着固有的组合复杂性挑战，传统设计方法难以有效应对，这促使研究者探索新的设计范式来自动化处理这种复杂性。

Method: 提出计算设计合成(CDS)框架，该框架利用深度学习和生成式AI技术自动创建新颖系统。通过两个案例研究进行验证：电驱动系统设计和空间尺寸标注问题。

Result: 两个案例研究成功证明了该方法的可行性，AI驱动的方法实现了从基于仿真的优化向最小人工监督的自主设计的根本性转变。

Conclusion: 自动化设计(AiD)作为一种变革性范式，能够有效应对现代高科技系统设计的组合复杂性，推动工程领域向自主设计方向发展，具有显著的实践价值。

Abstract: This article addresses the combinatorial complexity inherent in modern high-tech system design by presenting automation-in-design (AiD) as a transformative paradigm. We propose computational design synthesis (CDS), a framework utilising deep learning and generative AI to automate the creation of novel systems. Two case studies (e-drive system design and spatial dimensioning problem) serve as proof-points for this approach. The AI-driven methods used in the case studies represent a fundamental shift in engineering, advancing from simulation-based optimisation towards autonomous design with minimal human supervision.

</details>


### [4] [Internalizing the Future: A Unified Agentic Training Paradigm for World Model Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27483)
*Xuan Zhang,Zhijian Zhou,Lingfeng Qiao,Yulei Qin,Ke Li,Xing Sun,Xiaoyu Tan,Chao Qu,Yuan Qi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出三阶段训练范式（WM-AMT、FE-SFT、FC-RL），使LLM智能体具备"what-if"推理能力，在搜索和数学推理任务上优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体在长期任务中本质上是反应式的，缺乏内部世界模型来模拟未来结果；而人类通过"what-if"推理在承诺前评估潜在计划。研究发现简单微调存在"格式-能力差距"，仅产生浅层模仿而无真正的预测基础。

Method: 提出三阶段训练范式：(i)世界模型智能体中期训练(WM-AMT)向策略注入潜在预测能力；(ii)格式激发监督微调(FE-SFT)结构化已注入的能力；(iii)前瞻条件强化学习(FC-RL)优化生成模拟的校准和效用。训练单一自回归模型同时输出前瞻状态推演和计划条件的成功估计（文本形式的Q值）。

Result: 在搜索和数学推理任务上，该方法一致性地优于其他训练基线。证明了有效的内部世界建模需要能力优先的训练流程来实现有基础的、校准的前瞻能力。

Conclusion: LLM智能体中有效的内部世界建模需要能力优先的训练流程来达成有基础且校准的前瞻能力，而非简单的后训练微调。这为构建具有真正预测能力的智能体提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents have demonstrated strong capability in sequential decision-making, yet they remains fundamentally reactive in long-horizon tasks. Unlike humans who employ "what-if" reasoning to evaluate potential plans before commitment, standard agents lack an internal world model to simulate future outcomes. Therefore, we propose to internalize future-aware planning by training a single autoregressive model to verbalize both a prospective state rollout and a plan-conditioned success estimate-a textual analogue of the Q-value. Crucially, we identify a format-capability gap: simply fine-tuning agents on look-ahead traces during post-training leads to superficial mimicry of foresight without genuine predictive grounding. To bridge this gap, we introduce a three-stage training paradigm: (i) World Model Agentic Mid-Training (WM-AMT) to inject latent predictive capabilities into the policy; (ii) Format-Eliciting SFT (FE-SFT) to structure this injected capability; and (iii) Foresight-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning (FC-RL) to refine the calibration and utility of the generated simulations. Evaluated on search and mathematical reasoning tasks, our approach consistently outperforms other training baselines. Our results demonstrate that effective internal world modeling in LLM agents requires a capability-first training pipeline to achieve grounded and calibrated foresight.

</details>


### [5] [Odyssey: Constructing Verifiable Local Truth-Preserving Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27593)
*Sridhar Mahadevan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了ODYSSEY框架，通过可组合的铸造厂构建可验证的、局部真理保持的基础模型，并引入了基于范畴论的理论支撑和实际实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基础模型缺乏形式化的真理保持机制和可验证性，难以处理局部上下文的一致性和组合性。现有方法缺乏统一的数学框架来构建和维护可信的知识表示。因此需要一个基于范畴论的框架来形式化地构建可验证的、局部真理保持的基础模型组件。

Method: 1. 定义铸造厂作为基本构建模块，包含局部上下文覆盖、局部表示族、限制映射、粘合规则、阻碍策略、更新义务和人机视图
2. 将铸造厂形式化为携带论证组件的知识层
3. 引入通用铸造厂学习(UFL)，通过左Kan延拓构建候选铸造厂，右Kan延拓强制执行限制、粘合、阻碍和论证条件
4. 设计铸造厂SQL(FSQL)查询界面和TICKET认证机制
5. 在多个具体铸造厂上实现和测试框架

Result: 1. 实现了完整的ODYSSEY框架
2. 在多个具体铸造厂（证据/论证、运营决策、机构/金融、市场意义、科学挑战等）上验证了框架的有效性
3. 证明了相同的范畴机制可支持领域构建、工件重演、层诊断、基于Toulmin的审查、残留阻碍账本和因果声明提取
4. 成功应用于ICML 2026教程

Conclusion: ODYSSEY框架提供了基于范畴论的统一机制，实现了可验证的、局部真理保持的基础模型构建，为可信AI系统提供了形式化基础。该框架具有广泛的适用性，能够支持多样化的应用场景。

Abstract: We introduce a categorical framework called ODYSSEY for constructing verifiable, local truth-preserving foundation models as compositions of foundries: building-block architectural components that specify a cover of local contexts, local representation families, restriction maps, gluing rules, obstruction policies, update obligations, and human-facing views. A foundry is an organized sheaf of knowledge that carries within it an argumentation component. Concrete foundries are built from generic foundries such as evidence/argument, operational decision, institutional/financial, market meaning, scientific challenge, research-program, assistant-build, and evaluation-harness foundries. Universal Foundry Learning (UFL) formalizes foundry construction as a composition of left and right Kan extensions, with left Kan extension rolling local artifacts into candidate foundries and right Kan extension enforcing the restriction, gluing, obstruction, and argumentation conditions required for promotion. Foundry SQL (FSQL) is a small typed query surface for slicing maintained foundry artifacts that uses TICKET (Topos Integration using Causal Kan Extension Transformers) certification for admitting external or pre-built models into durable ODYSSEY state. ODYSSEY is fully implemented and tested across a wide spectrum of concrete foundries, showing that the same categorical machinery supports domain construction, artifact replay, sheaf diagnostics, grounded Toulmin/local-LLM scrutiny, residual-obstruction ledgers, and optimized TICKET-compatible causal-claim extraction across heterogeneous sources. This paper is to be presented as a 2.5 hour tutorial at ICML 2026. The tutorial home page is at https://bit.ly/4ajS0nA.

</details>


### [6] [DysLexLens: A Low-Resource LLM Framework for Analysing Dyslexic Learners Insights from Online Forums](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27619)
*Dana Rezazadegan,Atie Kia,Phongpadid Nandavong,Dominique Carlon,Jeremy Nguyen,Abhik Banerjee,James Marshall,Anthony McCosker,Yong-Bin Kang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了DysLexLens框架，一个面向低资源场景的LLM框架，用于分析阅读障碍学习者使用AI工具的体验，通过Reddit论坛数据实现了从噪声数据到可验证查询响应的端到端处理流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管阅读障碍学习者越来越多地使用AI工具辅助阅读、写作、组织和学习相关任务，但他们在使用这些工具时的真实体验在很大程度上仍未被研究。现有研究缺乏对这一特定用户群体与AI交互经验的深入分析，特别是在低资源在线论坛数据环境下提取有价值见解的方法。

Method: 提出了DysLexLens框架，包含四个核心特征：(1)采用字典驱动过滤方法构建聚焦于阅读障碍和AI的Reddit语料库，过滤噪声和弱相关帖子；(2)集成LLM辅助语义分析与知识图谱(KG)查询推理以发现有意义的模式；(3)建立量化评估指标(RAGAS和查询鲁棒性)衡量LLM生成响应的性能；(4)提供结构化定性验证指南，重点关注幻觉和证据对齐。整个架构实现了端到端、可追溯证据的处理流程。

Result: 使用阅读障碍相关的Reddit论坛数据和30个问题验证了DysLexLens的有效性，展示了其在低资源论坛数据环境中提取有价值见解的能力，并证明了该方法在其他低资源论坛数据场景中的潜在泛化能力。框架、样本数据、问题和评估结果已在GitHub开源以保证可复现性。

Conclusion: DysLexLens为分析阅读障碍学习者与AI工具交互体验提供了一个有效的技术框架，填补了低资源论坛数据分析方法论的空白，其设计理念和实现方法可推广应用于其他类似的低资源在线社区数据挖掘场景。

Abstract: Dyslexic learners increasingly use artificial intelligence (AI) tools to support reading, writing, organisation, and study-related tasks. However, their lived experiences with these tools remain largely underexamined. This paper proposes DysLexLens, a low-resource LLM framework, designed to analyse dyslexic learners experience with AI through online forum discussions. DysLexLens is designed as an end-to-end, evidence-traceable architecture which transforms noisy social media posts into a dictionary-driven corpora, provides knowledge-graph (KG)-based question reasoning, generates verifiable query responses, and enables response evaluation through quantitative and human-grounded assessment. DysLexLens has four key features. First, it employs a dictionary-driven filtering method to construct a more focused Reddit corpus on dyslexia and AI, filtering out noisy and weakly related posts to improve the relevance of data collected from low-resource forum contexts. Second, it integrates LLM-assisted semantic analysis with KG-based query reasoning to uncover meaningful patterns. Third, it has quantitative evaluation metrics (RAGAS and Query Robustness) to measure LLM-generated response performance. Fourth, it provides structured qualitative validation guidelines for assessing response quality, with a specific focus on hallucination and evidence alignment. We demonstrate the effectiveness of DysLexLens using dyslexia-related Reddit forum data and 30 questions. The results show its potential generalisability to other low-resource forum data contexts. DysLexLens, sample data, questions and evaluation results are available at Github to support reproducibility.

</details>


### [7] [MER-R1: Multimodal Emotion Reasoning via Slow-Fast Thinking Synergy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27652)
*Zhiyuan Han,Beier Zhu,Wenwen Tong,Chengwei Qin,Xinyi Wang,Jiayu Zhang,Jiangnan Chen,Hewei Guo,Dongchuan Ran,Lewei Lu,Xun Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现显式推理并不一定提升多模态情感识别准确率，提出MER-R1强化学习框架，通过解耦召回率和精确率的双重目标以及慢思考-快思考置信度校准，实现两者的联合优化，在基准测试上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究发现，基于推理的多模态大语言模型中，直接回答（快思考）往往优于深思熟虑后的推理（慢思考），尽管后者提高了可解释性。快思考倾向于更广泛、更自信的预测从而提升召回率，而慢思考则通过保守过滤错误类别来提升精确率。这种互补性促使作者探索如何将两者优势结合而非相互折衷。

Method: 提出MER-R1强化学习框架：（1）双重目标解耦：将召回率和精确率分离为两个独立优化信号，实现联合优化而非权衡；（2）慢思考-快思考置信度校准：将最终慢思考答案与快思考直觉对齐，强化正确情感类别并抑制错误类别；（3）提供理论证明，表明这种协同效应能缓解优化过程中的方差诱导干扰。

Result: 在MER-UniBench和MME-Emotion基准测试上进行广泛实验，MER-R1达到最先进性能，成功使推理真正促进情感识别任务。

Conclusion: MER-R1框架成功统一了快思考的召回导向直觉和慢思考的精确导向选择性，证明了通过合理的框架设计可以让显式推理真正有益于情感识别任务，为多模态情感识别领域提供了新的优化思路。

Abstract: We find that explicit reasoning does not necessarily translate into better multimodal emotion recognition (MER) accuracy, even though it makes predictions more interpretable. Specifically, for reasoning-based MLLMs, fast thinking by triggering direct answers often outperforms slow thinking after deliberative reasoning. Our empirical analyses show that fast thinking improves recall with broader and more confident predictions, whereas slow thinking favors precision through conservative filtering of incorrect categories. Building on these insights, we propose MER-R1, a reinforcement learning framework that turns slow-fast complementarity into explicit optimization. Dual-objective disentanglement separates recall and precision into two optimization signals, allowing them to be jointly optimized rather than traded off against each other. Slow-fast confidence calibration further aligns the final slow-thinking answer with fast-thinking intuition, strengthening correct emotions while suppressing incorrect ones. In this way, MER-R1 unifies the recall-oriented intuition of fast thinking with the precision-oriented selectivity of slow thinking. We further provide theoretical justification for this synergy, showing that it mitigates variance-induced interference during optimization. Extensive experiments on MER-UniBench and MME-Emotion show that MER-R1 achieves state-of-the-art performance and makes reasoning genuinely benefit emotion recognition.

</details>


### [8] [ToE: A Hierarchical and Explainable Claim Verification Framework with Dynamic Multi-source Evidence Retrieval and Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27736)
*Zhaoqi Wang,Zijian Zhang,Kun Zheng,Zhen Li,Xin Li,Chunlei Li,Jiamou Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Tree of Evidence (ToE)框架用于自动事实核查，通过层次化论证树建模声明，结合强化学习驱动的多源检索、证据评估和论证树聚合算法，在对抗性污染输入上相比基线提升4-24个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虚假新闻的快速传播对信息生态系统构成日益严重的威胁，特别是在生成式引擎优化(GEO)中毒攻击下，AI生成的虚假信息能够被检索系统系统性呈现，从而污染大语言模型的推理过程。

Method: 提出Tree of Evidence (ToE)层次化证据推理框架，将每个声明建模为动态扩展的论证树。该框架整合了强化学习驱动的多源检索智能体、证据评估智能体和论证树聚合算法，通过可解释的证据链迭代地分解、检索和验证声明。同时提供了检索过程的理论分析，推导出形式化误差界限，保证了学习策略收敛到信息论最优策略的邻域。

Result: 在多个数据集和骨干LLM上的实验表明，ToE相比竞争基线实现了4到24个百分点的提升，在对抗性污染输入上表现尤为突出。理论分析证明了检索策略收敛到信息论最优策略邻域的保证。

Conclusion: ToE框架有效解决了GEO中毒攻击下的自动事实核查问题，通过层次化证据推理和强化学习驱动的检索策略，显著提升了对对抗性虚假信息的检测能力，同时提供了理论收敛保证。

Abstract: The rapid spread of fake news poses increasing threats to information ecosystems, especially as AI-generated misinformation under Generative Engine Optimization (GEO) poisoning allows adversarially crafted content to be systematically surfaced by retrieval systems, contaminating LLM reasoning. In this paper, we propose Tree of Evidence (ToE), a hierarchical evidence reasoning framework for automated fact-checking that models each claim as a dynamically expanding argument tree. ToE integrates a reinforcement learning-driven multi-source retrieval agent, an evidence evaluation agent, and an argument tree aggregation algorithm to iteratively decompose, retrieve, and verify claims through an explainable evidence chain. We further provide a theoretical analysis of the retrieval process, deriving a formal error bound that guarantees the learned policy converges to a neighborhood of the information-theoretically optimal policy. Experiments across multiple datasets and backbone LLMs demonstrate that ToE achieves improvements ranging from 4 to 24 percentage points over competitive baselines, with particularly pronounced gains on adversarially poisoned inputs.

</details>


### [9] [Towards Reliable and Robust LLM Planning: Symbolic Feedback-Driven Iterative Self-Refinement Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27757)
*Jiajing Zhang,Jiamei Jiang,Chenyang Zhang,Feifei Mo,Linjing Li,Daniel Zeng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出符号反馈驱动的迭代自精炼框架，通过自然语言提示机制、符号验证器和规划识别器提升大语言模型在长时域规划任务中的鲁棒性和可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在学术界和工业界受到广泛关注，但其部署在鲁棒性和可靠性方面存在关键安全隐患。规划作为智能行为的核心组成部分，对大语言模型仍具有挑战性，由于固有复杂性，模型在长时域决策任务中经常产生不可行或错误的解决方案。

Method: 提出符号反馈驱动的迭代自精炼框架：1）引入自然语言提示机制，将逻辑符号映射为自然语言描述，使大语言模型更好地捕捉任务约束和语义；2）设计符号验证器识别错误并将其转化为大语言模型可解释的纠正指令，引导自精炼过程；3）利用规划识别器推断目标可达性，促进更有效的目标导向。

Result: 实证结果表明，该框架在长时域规划任务中持续提升可行性和正确性，验证了其在增强基于大语言模型规划的可靠性方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架有效提升了大语言模型在规划任务中的可靠性，具有实现更可信赖AI系统的潜力，为解决大语言模型部署中的安全性和可靠性问题提供了有前景的解决方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have attracted widespread attention from academia and industry, yet their deployment raises critical security concerns regarding robustness and reliability. Planning, a core component of intelligent behavior, remains challenging for LLMs, which often produce infeasible or incorrect solutions in long-horizon decision-making tasks due to inherent complexity. In this paper, we propose a symbolic feedback-driven iterative self-refinement framework to enhance the robustness and reliability of LLMs in long-horizon planning. Specifically, a natural language prompting mechanism is introduced to map logical symbols into natural language descriptions, enabling LLMs to better capture task constraints and semantics. We further design a symbolic verifier that identifies errors and converts them into corrective instructions interpretable by the LLM, thereby guiding self-refinement. In addition, we leverage a plan recognizer to infer goal reachability, facilitating more effective guidance toward desired goals. Empirical results demonstrate that the proposed framework consistently improves both feasibility and correctness in long-horizon planning tasks. This highlights its effectiveness in enhancing the reliability of LLM-based planning and potential to enable more trustworthy AI systems.

</details>


### [10] [Agent-Native Immune System: Architecture, Taxonomy, and Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28270)
*Bo Shen,Lifeng Chang,Tianyuan Wei,Yunpeng Li,Feng Shi,Yichen Han,Peijie Gao,Shiyi Kuang,Xin Chang,Dehui Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个生物启发的内源性防御架构——代理原生免疫系统（ANIS），通过六层免疫塔、统一病毒/疫苗分类法、监控三元组等创新，解决了自主代理在运行时面临的内存中毒、工具链操纵等安全威胁，填补了现有外部防御机制的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自主代理具备持久记忆、工具使用协议和多代理协作能力，极大地扩展了AI威胁景观。现有防御机制（如边界安全、训练时对齐）处于代理主动推理循环之外，无法有效防御运行时劫持攻击（如内存中毒、工具链操纵、多代理协议攻击），即使完全对齐的代理仍高度脆弱。

Method: 提出代理原生免疫系统（ANIS），构建六层免疫塔（L0-L5），其中L1为非认知的物理逻辑隔离层（屏障免疫）；建立代理病毒与代理疫苗的统一分类法，区分非参数化防御与参数化疫苗；设计监控三元组（Meta、Self、Auto）作为元认知自动化骨干，驱动持续免疫学习（CIL）。

Result: 成功构建了首个嵌入代理认知循环内部的生物启发式内源性防御架构；明确了模型对齐与代理免疫的理论分界：对齐是训练时静态的"宪法性"价值基础，ANIS是运行时动态的"执法"机制；提出了免疫协议标准化、自身免疫率（假阳性干预率）等新评估指标。

Conclusion: ANIS为自主代理提供了动态、内生的安全防护能力，填补了运行时防御空白，但仍面临免疫协议标准化、新型评估指标建立、集体智能生态中病原体与疫苗的协同进化动力学等开放挑战，为该领域指明了未来研究方向。

Abstract: The transition from static chat bots to autonomous agents--equipped with persistent memory, tool-use protocols, and multi-agent collaboration--has fundamentally expanded the AI threat landscape. Current defense mechanisms, such as perimeter security and training-time alignment, remain external to the agent's active reasoning loop. Consequently, they fall short: a fully aligned agent remains highly vulnerable to runtime hijacking via memory poisoning, tool-chain manipulation, or multi-agent protocol attacks. To address this critical gap, we introduce the Agent-Native Immune System (ANIS), the first biologically inspired, endogenous defense architecture embedded directly within the agent's cognitive loop. Our framework presents four primary contributions. First, we design a six-layer Immune Tower (L0-L5), distinctly incorporating Barrier Immunity (L1) as a non-cognitive, physical-and-logical isolation layer. Second, we establish a unified taxonomy of Agent Viruses and Agent Vaccines, formalizing the critical distinction between superficial non-parametric defenses and robust parametric vaccines. Third, we conceptualize the Harness Triad--Meta, Self, and Auto--a self-monitoring, meta-cognitive automation backbone that drives Continual Immune Learning (CIL), enabling vaccines to dynamically adapt to novel threats. Finally, we establish a rigorous theoretical demarcation between model alignment and agent immunity: while alignment provides a static "constitutional" value foundation during training, ANIS serves as the dynamic "law enforcement" mechanism during runtime. We conclude by framing open challenges for the field, including immune protocol standardization, novel evaluation metrics such as the Autoimmunity Rate (false-positive intervention rate), and the co-evolutionary dynamics between pathogens and vaccines within collective intelligence ecosystems.

</details>


### [11] [Understanding Rollout Error in Graph World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27780)
*Xinyuan Song,Zekun Cai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了图世界模型（GWMs）中的长视界推演误差问题，提出了统一的固定边和动态边GWM框架，并开发了图值推演界限来分离拓扑诱导放大和模型诱导放大。提出的Error-Aware GWM方法通过频谱正则化、推演一致性和关键节点加权，有效防止了长视界发散同时保持预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型常用于通过学习动态进行前向推演规划，但许多规划环境并非向量或图像，而是由智能体、工具、技能、路径和依赖关系组成的图结构。在这些场景中，局部预测误差可能保持局部或通过图传播，且当边被预测而非固定时，失效模式会发生变化。现有研究缺乏对图世界模型中长视界推演误差的系统分析。

Method: 提出了统一的固定边和动态边GWM框架，包含用于节点级、边级和图级决策的动作节点；开发了图值推演界限，将拓扑诱导放大与模型诱导放大分离；引入了动态边推演的联合节点-边算子；提出了Error-Aware GWM方法，结合频谱正则化、推演一致性和关键节点加权技术。

Result: 在合成拓扑和异构智能体图测试平台上的实验表明：推演误差和规划遗憾随视界增长；当结构演化时需要动态边训练；Error-Aware GWM防止了长视界发散同时保持预测精度。真实世界图基准测试显示GWMs在动态图推演和智能体规划方面最有用，而专门的图模型在静态或稀疏预测任务上仍然表现强劲。

Conclusion: 图世界模型在动态图推演和智能体规划场景中具有独特价值，Error-Aware GWM有效解决了长视界推演误差问题，为图结构环境中的规划和预测提供了可靠框架。该研究明确了GWMs的适用范围和局限性。

Abstract: World models are often used for planning by rolling learned dynamics forward. Many planning environments, however, are not vectors or images; they are graphs of agents, tools, skills, routes, and dependencies. In these settings, a local prediction error may stay local or spread through the graph, and the failure mode changes again when edges are predicted rather than fixed. This paper studies long-horizon rollout error in Graph World Models (GWMs). We formulate a unified fixed-edge and dynamic-edge GWM framework with action nodes for node-, edge-, and graph-level decisions. We develop graph-valued rollout bounds that separate topology-induced amplification from model-induced amplification, and we introduce a joint node-edge operator for dynamic-edge rollouts. Guided by the analysis, we propose Error-Aware GWM, which combines spectral regularization, rollout consistency, and critical-node weighting. Across synthetic topologies and heterogeneous agent-graph testbeds, rollout error and planning regret grow with horizon, dynamic-edge training is needed when structure evolves, and Error-Aware GWM prevents long-horizon divergence while preserving prediction accuracy. Real-world graph benchmarks clarify the scope of GWMs: they are most useful for dynamic graph rollout and agent planning, while specialized graph models remain strong on static or sparse prediction tasks.

</details>


### [12] [Grounded Iterative Language Planning: How Parameterized World Models Reduce Hallucination Propagation in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27806)
*Xinyuan Song,Zekun Cai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出GILP框架，结合参数化世界模型与LLM推理，将幻觉状态率从0.176降至0.035，成功率从0.668提升至0.838。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言智能体的世界模型存在两类：基于智能体的世界模型调用LLM API灵活推理，但错误表现为难以用回归损失评分的幻觉状态变化；参数化世界模型是训练的转移预测器，错误易于用NodeMSE等指标衡量，但作为独立规划器较弱。

Method: 提出Grounded Iterative Language Planning (GILP)，仅训练小型参数化骨干并与API智能体推理结合。骨干提供有效动作、预测状态变化、风险和价值；LLM起草动作和想象的delta；一致性门控在两者不一致时请求修订。

Result: 在GPT-4o-mini调用中，GILP将幻觉状态率从0.176降至0.035。在校准模拟器消融实验中，成功率从0.668提升至0.838，仅增加约22%的额外LLM调用。

Conclusion: GILP成功融合了参数化世界模型的可测量性与LLM的灵活推理能力，显著降低了幻觉状态率并提升了规划成功率，以较小的额外计算成本实现了两类世界模型的优势互补。

Abstract: World models for language agents come in two useful forms. An agent-based world model calls an LLM API and reasons flexibly in language, but its errors appear as hallucinated state changes that are hard to score with ordinary regression losses. A parameterized world model is a trained transition predictor; its errors are easier to measure with quantities such as NodeMSE, delta accuracy, and validity accuracy, but it is usually weaker as a standalone planner. We compare these two families on four graph-structured planning benchmarks and introduce operational hallucination metrics for the agent-based case. The comparison motivates \textbf{Grounded Iterative Language Planning} (GILP), which trains only a small parameterized backbone and combines it with API-based agent reasoning. The backbone supplies valid actions, predicted state deltas, risk, and value; the LLM drafts an action and imagined delta; and a consistency gate asks for revision when the two disagree. On real GPT-4o-mini calls, GILP reduces hallucinated-state rate from 0.176 to 0.035. In calibrated simulator ablations, it raises success from 0.668 to 0.838 while adding only ~22% extra LLM calls.

</details>


### [13] [ATOD: Annealed Turn-aware On-policy Distillation for Multi-turn Autonomous Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27814)
*Qitai Tan,Zefang Zong,Yang Li,Peng Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ATOD是一种混合在线蒸馏算法，通过退火调度结合OPD和RL的优势，并引入T-DUR机制增强长轨迹监督，在三个基准测试上平均成功率相比OPD提升3.03点，相比GRPO提升23.62点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小语言模型在长视野交互任务中需要快速模仿和奖励驱动改进，但现有方法存在互补性缺陷：在线策略蒸馏(OPD)早期改进快但性能上限受限(学生逼近教师后增益饱和)；强化学习(RL)能探索更高奖励上限但早期学习效率低(反馈稀疏延迟)。

Method: 提出ATOD算法：(1)退火OPD-RL调度：早期OPD主导以接近教师行为，逐步增强RL驱动基于奖励的探索；(2)引入回合级分歧-不确定性重加权(T-DUR)：软性放大高效用回合，改善长轨迹中的密集监督。

Result: 在ALFWorld、WebShop和Search-QA三个基准上，三种学生模型尺寸平均：相比OPD成功率提升3.03点，相比GRPO提升23.62点，超越对应教师模型2.16点。

Conclusion: ATOD成功利用OPD与RL的互补性，通过退火调度和回合级重加权有效解决了小模型在长视野任务中训练效率与性能上限的平衡问题，为小语言模型后训练提供了新思路。

Abstract: Training small language-model agents for long-horizon interactive tasks requires both fast imitation and reward-driven improvement. On-policy distillation (OPD) provides dense teacher guidance and typically improves rapidly in the early stage, but its gains saturate once the student approaches the teacher, limiting the final performance ceiling. Reinforcement learning (RL) directly optimizes environment rewards and encourages exploratory improvement toward a higher reward-defined ceiling, but sparse and delayed feedback makes early-stage learning much less efficient than OPD. In this paper, we propose ATOD (Annealed Turn-aware On-policy Distillation), a hybrid online distillation algorithm that explicitly exploits this complementarity. (1) ATOD uses an annealed OPD-RL schedule: OPD dominates early training to approach teacher-level behavior, while RL is gradually strengthened to drive reward-based exploration. (2) ATOD introduces Turn-level Disagreement-Uncertainty Reweighting (T-DUR), which softly amplifies high-utility turns and improves dense supervision in long trajectories. Experiments on ALFWorld, WebShop, and Search-QA show that ATOD consistently outperforms competing post-training baselines: across the three student sizes, ATOD improves average success rate by 3.03 points over OPD and 23.62 points over GRPO, while surpassing the corresponding teacher models by 2.16 points.

</details>


### [14] [NormAct: A Benchmark for Hidden Social Norm Compliance in Embodied Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27826)
*Shiyun Zhao,Xinwei Song,Tianyu Guo,Xiaomeng Gao,Mingyuan Liu,Xu Han,Yuanyuan Zhang,Zhenliang Zhang,Xue Feng,Bo Dai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出NormAct基准测试，评估多模态大语言模型在具身规划中推理隐藏社会规范的能力。实验发现模型显式目标达成率为67.3%，但隐藏规范遵循率仅26.4%。提出的NormPerceptor方法将任务成功率从24.2%提升至46.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估主要关注显式目标达成或直接的规范知识，很少评估规划器能否在动作序列中推理并应用隐藏的社会规范约束。隐式社会规范往往对显式最优行为施加限制，这对于具身智能体的社交适宜性至关重要。

Method: 1. 构建NormAct基准测试，将隐藏规范嵌入普通任务中，从目标达成、规范遵循和整体任务成功三个维度评估规划质量。
2. 设计线索条件实验，探究规范遵循差距的根源。
3. 提出NormPerceptor框架，这是一个上下文条件的线索生成器，在规划前推理场景相关规范。

Result: 1. 先进MLLMs（GPT-5.4、Claude Opus 4.7、Gemini 3 Pro）在显式目标达成率达67.3%，但隐藏规范遵循率仅26.4%，存在显著差距。
2. 线索条件实验表明，差距源于在上下文中激活和落地相关规范的挑战，而非缺乏一般社会知识。
3. NormPerceptor将任务成功率从24.2%提升至46.7%，接近翻倍。

Conclusion: 研究结果强调了使具身智能体主动检测隐藏规范、将其落地到视觉证据并作为动作规划约束加以整合的重要性。规范激活与落地是实现社交适宜具身规划的关键能力。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are increasingly deployed as embodied planners in egocentric environments, where task success requires not only achieving instructed goals but also acting in socially appropriate ways. While explicit goals may render certain actions optimal, implicit social norms often impose hidden constraints. Existing evaluations typically focus on explicit goal achievement or direct norm knowledge, seldom assessing whether planners can infer and apply these hidden constraints within action sequences. We introduce NormAct, a benchmark for embodied social-norm interactions that evaluates plans on Goal Achievement, Norm Compliance, and overall Task Success. NormAct uniquely embeds hidden norms within ordinary tasks, testing whether models can realize them without explicit instruction. Experiments with state-of-the-art MLLMs (GPT-5.4, Claude Opus 4.7, Gemini 3 Pro) reveal a significant gap: models achieve explicit goals in 67.3\% of cases, but comply with hidden norms in only 26.4\%. Cue-condition experiments indicate that this gap stems not from a lack of general social knowledge, but from challenges in activating and grounding relevant norms in context. To address this, we propose NormPerceptor, a context-conditioned cue generator that infers scene-relevant norms prior to planning, increasing Task Success from 24.2\% to 46.7\%. Our results underscore the importance of enabling embodied agents to proactively detect hidden norms, ground them in visual evidence, and integrate them as action-planning constraints. Our benchmark is publicly available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/Caleb196x/NormAct.

</details>


### [15] [Verifiable Geometry Problem Solving: Solver-Driven Autoformalization and Theorem Proposing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27926)
*Can Li,Ting Zhang,Junbo Zhao,Hua Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了SD-GPS框架，通过将符号求解器作为执行预言机集成到自动形式化和定理预测两个阶段，显著提升了几何问题求解的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前神经符号几何问题求解框架存在两个关键瓶颈：自动形式化阶段将多模态翻译视为与下游求解器兼容性解耦的静态任务；定理预测阶段因固定规则库导致求解器频繁陷入演绎僵局。

Method: 提出SD-GPS求解器驱动框架，包含两个核心组件：(1)求解器驱动的自动形式化，在QwenVL3-2B基础上统一了监督式形式语言适应性和可解性引导的强化学习，将可执行性作为核心训练信号；(2)验证定理提出，引入僵局感知代理从当前证明状态提出局部辅助引理，并通过符号验证过滤所有提案以确保正确性。

Result: 在Geometry3K和PGPS9K数据集上的实验表明，SD-GPS在标准补全、多选和跨模态参考任务中均持续优于现有的MLLM、神经和神经符号方法。

Conclusion: 闭环连接多模态感知与符号执行显著改善了几何推理能力，为神经智能体如何通过形式系统接地实现可验证的问题求解能力提供了深刻见解。

Abstract: Geometry Problem Solving have increasingly adopt the neuro-symbolic paradigm, combining neural intuition with symbolic rigor. However, current frameworks suffer from severe bottlenecks in two core stages: autoformalization, which treats multimodal translation as a static task decoupled from downstream solver compatibility, and theorem prediction, where solvers frequently hit a deductive impasse due to fixed rule libraries. To address these, we propose SD-GPS, a solver-driven framework that treats the symbolic solver as an execution oracle throughout both formalization and deduction. First, Solver-Driven Autoformalization unifies supervised formal-language adaptation and solvability-guided reinforcement learning into a single module built on QwenVL3-2B, making executability the central training signal. Second, Verified Theorem Proposing introduces an impasse-aware agent that proposes local auxiliary lemmas from current proof states, ensuring soundness by filtering all proposals through symbolic verification. Empirical evaluations on Geometry3K and PGPS9K demonstrate that SD-GPS consistently outperforms existing MLLM, neural, and neuro-symbolic methods across standard completion, multiple-choice, and cross-modal reference regimes, proving that closing the loop between multimodal perception and symbolic execution significantly improves geometric reasoning, offering profound insights into how neural agents can be grounded by formal systems to achieve verifiable problem-solving capabilities.

</details>


### [16] [RelBall: Relation Ball with Quaternion Rotation for Knowledge Graph Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27967)
*Yike Liu,Peijia Xie,Chao He,Huiling Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了RelBall模型，通过引入模数变换和尾中心关系球，解决了现有知识图谱补全模型无法同时处理非交换组合模式、语义层次结构和一对多关系的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界的知识图谱通常是不完整的，知识图谱补全（KGC）旨在利用已知三元组预测缺失的链接。现有模型如RotatE无法处理非交换组合模式，Rotate3D虽然解决了该问题但仍无法捕获知识图谱中普遍存在的语义层次结构，且两者都无法有效建模一对多关系。

Method: 提出了RelBall模型，在Rotate3D基础上引入两个创新：1）模数变换用于建模层次结构，驱动抽象概念趋向较小的模数、具体实例趋向较大的模数；2）尾中心关系球用于建模一对一、一对多、多对一和多对多关系。

Result: RelBall能够覆盖所有关系模式（包括对称、反对称、反转、组合和语义层次），提供可解释的层次表示（模数直接反映语义级别），并支持一对一、一对多、多对一和多对多关系。在多个数据集上的实验表明，RelBall在各种基线模型上具有竞争力的链接预测性能。

Conclusion: RelBall成功解决了现有知识图谱补全模型的关键局限性，在保持竞争力的链接预测性能的同时，实现了对所有关系模式、语义层次结构和复杂关系类型的全面建模能力。

Abstract: Real-world knowledge graphs are often incomplete, lacking many valid facts. Knowledge Graph Completion (KGC) aims to predict missing links using known triples, thereby enhancing graph coverage. A key challenge is modeling diverse relational patterns such as symmetry, antisymmetry, inversion, composition and semantic hierarchy. Existing models such as RotatE can capture symmetric, antisymmetric, inverse, and commutative composition patterns, yet struggle with non-commutative composition. Rotate3D addresses this by introducing non-commutativity via three-dimensional rotations, but still fails to capture the semantic hierarchies prevalent in knowledge graphs. Moreover, both models cannot effectively model one-to-many relations. To overcome these limitations, we propose RelBall, which extends Rotate3D with two innovations. First, our model introduces modulus transformation to model hierarchies, driving abstract concepts toward smaller moduli and concrete instances toward larger ones. Second, it introduces a tail-centric relation ball to model one-to-one, one-to-many, many-to-one, and many-to-many relations. RelBall offers the following advantages: (1) coverage of all relational patterns, including the ones mentioned above; (2) an interpretable hierarchical representation where the modulus directly reflect semantic levels; (3) support for one-to-one, one-to-many, many-to-one, and many-to-many relations. Experiments on multiple datasets demonstrate RelBall's competitive link prediction performance against various baselines.

</details>


### [17] [Lifted Causal Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28024)
*Malte Luttermann,Tanya Braun,Ralf Möller,Marcel Gehrke*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了参数化因果因子图（PCFG）和提升因果推理算法（LCI），通过在关系域中利用不可区分性来显著加速因果效应计算，并扩展到处理部分因果知识的场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的因果推理在关系域中计算因果效应时效率低下。现有的提升推理技术虽然在概率图模型中能利用不可区分性加速查询，但尚未应用于因果推理。本文旨在将提升推理技术扩展到因果推理领域，以在保持精确结果的同时大幅提升计算效率。

Method: 1) 引入参数化因果因子图（PCFG），在提升模型中融入因果知识并给出干预的形式化语义；2) 提出提升因果推理算法（LCI），在提升层面计算因果效应；3) 提出部分有向参数化因果因子图（PD-PCFG）作为PCFG的推广，用于处理部分因果知识，并扩展LCI算法以适用于PD-PCFG。

Result: LCI算法相比命题化推理方法（如因果贝叶斯网络）能够显著加速因果推理；PD-PCFG框架扩展了提升因果推理的适用范围，使其能够处理更广泛的模型且需要更少的先验因果知识。

Conclusion: 本文成功将提升推理技术应用于因果推理领域，在关系域中实现了高效的因果效应计算，同时降低了对完整因果知识的依赖，拓宽了提升因果推理的应用场景。

Abstract: Lifted inference exploits indistinguishabilities in probabilistic graphical models by using a representative for indistinguishable objects, thereby speeding up query answering while maintaining exact answers. In this article, we show how lifting can be applied to efficiently compute causal effects in relational domains. More specifically, we introduce parametric causal factor graphs (PCFGs) to incorporate causal knowledge in lifted models and give a formal semantics of interventions therein. We further present the Lifted Causal Inference (LCI) algorithm to compute causal effects on a lifted level, thereby drastically speeding up causal inference compared to propositional inference, e.g., in causal Bayesian networks. In addition, we present partially directed parametric causal factor graphs (PD-PCFGs) as a generalisation of PCFGs to handle partial causal knowledge and extend LCI to perform lifted causal inference in a PD-PCFG, thereby extending the applicability of lifted causal inference to a broader range of models requiring less prior knowledge about causal relationships.

</details>

*Oxygen AIIC,Chan Long,Chao Liu,Chaofan Chen,Chaohui Dong,Chunyuan Guo,Danping Liu,Debin Liu,Deping Xiang,Fulai Xu,Guangyue Liu,Hao Li,Huichun Hu,Jian Yang,Jianan Wang,Jianbo Zhao,Jiaoyang Li,Jiaxing Wang,Jinglong Li,Jinjin Guo,Jun Fang,Jun Liu,Kai Zhou,Li Wang,Lili Gao,Liying Chen,Luning Yang,Mengdi Zhou,Pengzhang Liu,Qi Lv,Qianyun Wang,Qixia Jiang,Ruyue Li,Shimu Liang,Shuxing Wang,Sijie Zhang,Siqi Li,Tianhao Gao,Wang Ke,Weihu Huang,Wencan Lai,Wenjie Zhang,Xiaohui Zhang,Xiaojing Dong,Ya Liu,Yifeng Zhang,Yixiang Wang,Yongtai Zhang,Yongyi Liao,Zhaoru Chen,Zhen Chen,Zhiyong Ma,Zhiyuan Liu,Zhongwei Liu,Ziyan Xing*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 京东推出基于大模型和视觉语言模型的工业级商品知识生产与服务平合Oxygen AIIC，通过本体工程、S2D架构、自演化模型和统一商品通道四大支柱，实现94.2%精确率和82.8%召回率的商品知识生产，在搜索、推荐等场景中取得显著成效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 京东作为全球最大电商平台之一，服务超7亿活跃用户和数百万商家，拥有数百亿SKU。在此规模下，高质量结构化商品知识对提升用户体验、降低管理成本和提高运营效率至关重要，但面临三大工业级挑战：概念快速涌现、海量SKU高质量知识生产、多样化下游需求。

Method: 构建四大核心支柱：(1)人机协同驱动的本体工程，支持百万级条目本体的动态演进与敏捷扩展；(2)"语义搜索后判别"(S2D)知识识别架构，结合吞吐量提升策略，实现可扩展、可延伸、高吞吐的AI商品库生产；(3)自演化商品理解LLM/VLM，稳定可控地持续改进；(4)统一商品通道作为数据与服务枢纽。

Result: 模型在知识生产中达到94.2%精确率和82.8%召回率；覆盖数万京东品类，日均处理数亿商品更新，累计数千亿商品知识资产；搜索流量覆盖率达80.4%，商品信息质量问题下降37%，商品上架时核心属性自动填充率超80%。

Conclusion: Oxygen AIIC成功部署于华为昇腾NPU，覆盖搜索、推荐、运营、品类规划等核心业务场景，在大规模工业应用中实现了可衡量的收益，证明了基于LLM/VLM的商品知识生产与服务平台的可行性和有效性。

Abstract: JD.com, one of the world's largest e-commerce platforms, serves over 700 million active users and millions of merchants, with a catalog of tens of billions of SKUs. At this scale, high-quality, structured item knowledge underpins a better consumer experience, lower management costs, and higher operational efficiency-yet producing and serving it poses three industrial-scale challenges: fast-emerging concepts, high-quality knowledge production for massive SKUs, and diverse downstream requirements. To address these challenges, we present the JD Oxygen AI Item Center (Oxygen AIIC), an industrial-scale platform built on LLMs/VLMs for item-knowledge production and service. Oxygen AIIC is built around four core pillars: (i) ontology engineering driven by efficient human-AI collaboration, which supports the dynamic evolution and agile expansion of an ontology with millions of entries; (ii) a "Semantic Search then Discrimination"(S2D) knowledge identification architecture that, combined with throughput improvement strategies, enables scalable, extensible, and high-throughput AI Item Library production for tens of billions of SKUs; (iii) self-evolving item-understanding LLMs/VLMs that improve in a stable and controllable manner, enabling knowledge production with 94.2% precision and 82.8% recall; and (iv) a unified item tunnel that serves as the data and service hub. Oxygen AIIC now covers tens of thousands of JD categories and processes hundreds of millions of item updates per day on Huawei Ascend NPUs. It has accumulated hundreds of billions of item-knowledge assets. Deployed across core business scenarios-including search, recommendation, operations, category planning-Oxygen AIIC has delivered measurable gains at scale. Search-traffic coverage reaches 80.4%, item-information quality issues drop by 37%, the automated fill rate of core attributes during item listing exceeds 80%.

</details>


### [19] [Ontology-Guided Evidence Path Inference for Multi-hop Knowledge Graph Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28076)
*Yongxue Shan,Meihan Wu,Cundi Fang,Jie Peng,Xiaodong Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出OPI框架，通过引入关系中心本体图和双向检索机制解决多跳知识图谱问答中的搜索空间爆炸和语义约束不匹配问题，在多个数据集上显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多跳知识图谱问答方法主要依赖主题中心扩展，面临两大挑战：搜索空间随噪声混合类型路径快速增长，以及检索到的路径可能无法满足复杂问题的语义约束。

Method: 提出OPI框架：1）构建关系中心本体图捕获关系的头尾类型约束；2）设计双向检索机制，将预测答案类型映射到兼容的最终跳关系，结合主题侧前缀扩展与答案侧最终跳匹配；3）采用迭代精化策略重新评估路径和候选答案，过滤类型兼容但问题无关的证据。

Result: 在WebQSP上Hit@1/F1相比最强基线提升4.6/5.0个点，在CWQ上提升8.9/3.3个点，在MetaQA上仅用检索模块即可达到接近饱和的Hit@1性能，同时大幅减少搜索空间。

Conclusion: OPI框架通过本体引导的证据路径推理有效解决了多跳KGQA中的核心挑战，双向检索机制和迭代精化策略显著提升了问答准确性和效率。

Abstract: Knowledge graph question answering (KGQA) aims to answer natural-language questions by reasoning over structured facts. Existing multi-hop KGQA methods mainly rely on topic-centered expansion, which faces two key challenges: the search space rapidly grows with noisy mixed-type paths, and retrieved paths may fail to satisfy the semantic constraints of complex questions. To address these challenges, we propose OPI, an ontology-guided evidence path inference framework for multi-hop KGQA. OPI introduces a relation-centric ontology graph to capture the head-tail type constraints of relations, providing a compact interface for answer-side constraints. Based on this ontology graph, OPI first introduces a bidirectional retrieval mechanism by mapping the predicted answer type to compatible final-hop relations and combining topic-side prefix expansion with answer-side final-hop matching, thereby suppressing noisy mixed-type expansion. OPI further adopts an iterative refinement strategy to reassess retrieved paths and candidate answers under the question context, filtering type-compatible but question-irrelevant evidence for more reliable answer prediction. Experiments on WebQSP, CWQ, and MetaQA show that OPI substantially reduces the search space, improves Hit@1/F1 by 4.6/5.0 points on WebQSP and 8.9/3.3 points on CWQ over the strongest prior results, and achieves near-saturated Hit@1 on MetaQA with the retrieval module alone.

</details>


### [20] [Tandem Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28166)
*Difan Jiao,Raghav Singhal,Robert West,Ashton Anderson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Tandem强化学习（TRL）方法，通过强弱模型交替协作生成推理过程，在保持强模型独立推理能力的同时，显著提升了弱模型的可理解性和协作鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的可验证奖励强化学习（RLVR）虽然能训练出高性能模型，但其推理模式往往演变为难以理解的特殊模式（如可读性差、语言混合），导致弱代理和人类难以利用这些能力。这种兼容性问题阻碍了强模型推理能力在实际场景中的有效应用。

Method: 提出Tandem强化学习（TRL）框架：将训练中的强模型与冻结的弱模型随机交替协作生成推理链，对生成结果进行奖励，并使用标准GRPO损失函数优化强模型。该机制推动强模型产生弱模型能够理解的推理模式。

Result: 在Qwen3-4B-Instruct上以竞赛数学为任务进行训练，TRL在独立推理能力上与标准GRPO性能相当，同时涌现出三个特性：更强的弱模型交接鲁棒性、相对弱模型更小的分布漂移、对弱模型更易理解的思维链。

Conclusion: TRL为RLVR提供了一条有前景的技术路线，能够在不牺牲强模型推理性能的前提下，实现更好的多模型通信和人类兼容性，具有重要的实际应用价值。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has significantly improved the reasoning capability of large language models, reaching expert or even superhuman performance in domains such as competition math. However, whether weaker agents and humans can actually harness this capability is far less certain, with RLVR documented to drift reasoning toward idiosyncratic patterns such as poor readability and language mixing. Tandem training is a recently introduced paradigm that targets this compatibility problem: a trained, stronger senior co-generates each rollout with a frozen, weaker junior, and the two are rewarded as a team, so the senior is pushed to reason in ways the junior can follow. Yet this paradigm has so far been demonstrated only in proof-of-concept settings, leaving open whether it scales to the long chains of thought of the modern RLVR pipeline. In this work, we propose Tandem Reinforcement Learning (TRL), which carries the tandem training paradigm into RLVR. In TRL, the senior and a frozen junior alternate stochastically to co-generate the reasoning, the resulting generation is rewarded, and the standard GRPO loss is applied to the senior. Training Qwen3-4B-Instruct on competition math, we find that TRL matches vanilla GRPO on solo reasoning capability while three properties emerge together from the same rollout structure: stronger handoff robustness with the junior, reduced distributional drift from the junior, and a chain-of-thought more legible to the junior. Our results demonstrate a promising route for RLVR with practical payoffs in multi-model communication and human compatibility.

</details>

### [21] [SidConArena: An Environment Evaluating Agents in Open-Ended,Positive-Sum Bargaining Game](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27397)
*Yeqi Feng,Yuxin Chen,Tianxing He*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了SidConArena，一个用于在开放式、正和博弈场景下评估LLM代理的基准框架，发现前沿模型虽能获得更好经济结果，但仍存在资源误估、谈判被动和长期规划能力不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM代理评估环境主要局限于静态推理和零和博弈，无法反映现实世界经济互动的复杂特征。现实世界的经济互动通常是开放式的、混合动机的，代理需要进行谈判、创造正和剩余、竞争稀缺资产以及在延迟回报下进行规划，因此需要新的评估框架。

Method: 提出了SidConArena基准框架，将多人经济体形式化为有限视野的部分可观测随机博弈，包含三个耦合阶段：自然语言谈判与约束交易、基于确定性转换器的生产、以及长期资产的密封拍卖。框架结合了结构化观察、阶段感知的代理调度、神经符号动作接口和异步执行，既支持自由形式交互又保持了基于规则的评估。

Result: 在同质和异质锦标赛实验中，更强的前沿模型能够获得更高的经济成果，但代理仍表现出资源误估、被动谈判以及长期视野投资规划能力受限等问题。

Conclusion: SidConArena成功实现了在开放式正和谈判场景下对LLM代理的评估，揭示了当前前沿模型在经济推理方面的进步与局限性，为未来改进LLM代理的长期规划和谈判能力提供了重要的基准测试工具。

Abstract: Evaluating LLM agents requires dynamic environments that go beyond static reasoning and zero-sum games. Real-world economic interaction is often open-ended and mixed-motive: agents must negotiate, create positive-sum surplus, compete for scarce assets, and plan under delayed returns. We introduce SidConArena, a new benchmark framework for evaluating LLM agents in open-ended, positive-sum bargaining. SidConArena formalizes a multi-player economy as a finite-horizon partially observable stochastic game with three coupled phases: natural-language negotiation with binding trades, deterministic converter-based production, and sealed-bid auctions for long-term assets. The framework combines structured observations, phase-aware agent dispatching, a neural-symbolic action interface, and asynchronous execution, enabling free-form interaction while preserving rule-grounded evaluation. Across homogeneous and heterogeneous tournaments, stronger frontier models achieve higher economic outcomes, yet agents still misvalue resources, bargain passively, and remain limited in long-horizon investment planning.

</details>


### [22] [Delayed Verification Destabilizes Multi-Agent LLM Belief: Instability Thresholds and Optimal Corrector Placement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27409)
*Igor Itkin*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文通过图谱理论分析多智能体LLM系统中的幻觉传播问题，发现验证延迟会导致系统稳定性的临界阈值，并提出最优校正节点部署策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM系统中，验证器（verifier）和批评者（critic）智能体用于抑制幻觉，但验证存在延迟。在此延迟期间，虚假声明可能在智能体网络中传播，导致系统不稳定。

Method: 1. 将问题建模为带有接地校正节点的图上的延迟共识过程；
2. 使用接地拉普拉斯矩阵进行谱分解，推导验证剂量的闭环稳定性阈值；
3. 提出超模放置目标函数和贪婪近似算法，用于有限校正预算的最优节点分配。

Result: 1. 发现验证剂量存在稳定性阈值：校正过强或延迟过长会使共识转变为振荡；
2. 最不稳定区域发生在通信延迟与验证延迟重合时，延迟为2时阈值为逆黄金比例；
3. 五个开源模型实验证实了预测的剂量-延迟振荡现象；
4. 基于事实的答案生成使真值成为吸收边界，消除了这种效应。

Conclusion: 这种不稳定性特定于符号信念任务，而基于事实的验证保持稳定。研究为多智能体LLM系统的验证机制设计提供了理论指导，并给出了最优校正资源分配的实用算法。

Abstract: Multi-agent large language model (LLM) systems often rely on verifier and critic agents to suppress hallucinations, but verification is delayed. During this delay, false claims can propagate through the agent network. We model this process as delayed consensus on a graph with grounded corrector nodes. Spectral decomposition by the grounded Laplacian yields a closed-form stability threshold for the verification dose: correction that is too strong or too delayed can turn consensus into oscillation. The most unstable regime occurs when the communication and verification delays coincide; for delay two, the threshold is the inverse golden ratio. The same framework gives a supermodular placement objective and a greedy (1-1/e)-approximation rule for assigning a limited corrector budget to influential nodes. Experiments across five open models confirm the predicted dose-delay oscillations. By contrast, grounded factual answering makes truth an absorbing boundary and eliminates the effect, suggesting that the instability is specific to signed-belief tasks while grounded verification remains stabilizing

</details>


### [23] [Glite ARF: Verifier-Driven Research with Parallel LLM Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27416)
*Vassili Philippov,Pavel Katunin,Dmitry Andreev,Igor Ostanin,Anton Nikolaev*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出了Glite ARF框架，通过验证器驱动的研究方法实现了LLM编码代理在大型研究项目中的并行可复现运行，在BEA 2026词汇难度共享任务中取得优异成绩，并显著降低了基线RMSE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然LLM编码代理使自动化实证研究变得诱人，但简单的任务委托无法扩展到大型项目，因为低频率的指令失误会累积成损坏、不可复现的研究成果，这是当前亟待解决的问题。

Method: 提出Glite ARF开源Python框架，采用三角色架构：人类研究者选择假设、编码代理（Claude Code、Codex CLI）在固定结构下实现任务、确定性Python验证器脚本强制任务隔离、已完成工作的不可变性、修正覆盖和项目概览。验证器驱动研究的规则存在于代码中，违规时会大声失败，而非仅要求代理遵循的文本说明。

Result: 在BEA 2026词汇难度共享任务中，封闭赛道第一名、开放赛道第二名（西班牙语、德语、普通话三种目标语言），RMSE分别降低29.9%（封闭）和35.9%（开放）。执行了273个跟踪任务（146次实验运行），涉及129个特征集，最多12个并行代理，成本约450美元（LLM API）+498美元（第三方总计），成功识别并移除了4个目标泄漏特征集，将不合理的RMSE从0.609修正为0.802。框架的结构机制仅增加约1%的实际运行时间。

Conclusion: Glite ARF框架通过验证器驱动的研究方法成功实现了LLM编码代理在大规模研究项目中的可扩展、可复现和可审计运行，证明了在保持低时间开销（~1%）的同时能够显著提升研究效率和成果质量，为自动化实证研究提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: LLM coding agents make it tempting to automate empirical research by delegating experiments to them directly, but naive delegation does not scale to large projects: low-rate instruction lapses compound into broken, irreproducible artefacts. To address this problem, we present Glite ARF, an open-source Python framework for running many LLM coding agents in parallel on a research repository without sacrificing reproducibility or auditability. The framework defines a three-role stack: a human researcher chooses which hypotheses to test, coding agents (Claude Code, Codex CLI) implement individual tasks under a fixed structure, and deterministic Python verifier scripts enforce task isolation, immutability of completed work, a corrections overlay, and a materialised project overview. We call this verifier-driven research: the rules of the research process live in code that fails loudly when violated, not in prose that agents are merely asked to follow. Using Glite ARF, we developed our submission to the BEA 2026 vocabulary-difficulty shared task, placing first in the closed track and second in the open track on all three target languages (Spanish, German, Mandarin) and reducing the official baseline RMSE by 29.9% (closed) and 35.9% (open). The campaign comprised 273 tracked tasks (146 experiment runs) across 129 feature sets, run by up to twelve parallel agents orchestrated from a single laptop - with some model training on rented A100s - at approximately \$450 in LLM API spend (\$498 total third-party cost), and structured per-fold provenance let us catch and strip four target-leaking feature sets, correcting an implausible 0.609 RMSE to 0.802. Across three campaigns in three domains, the framework's structural machinery adds only about 1% of wall-clock time. Framework and a public demo project accompany this paper.

</details>


### [24] [QueenBee Planner: Skill-Evolving Communication Topologies for Token-Efficient LLM Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27492)
*Congjia Tian,Yuhang Yao,Jiaming Cui*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了QueenBee Planner框架，通过将多智能体系统的通信拓扑作为可学习的设计技能，实现了优于固定拓扑结构的性能提升，在CF任务上将RMSE从12.53降至7.87，同时降低了通信开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型多智能体系统的性能不仅依赖于单个智能体的推理能力，还高度依赖于智能体之间的连接方式。现有的固定拓扑结构无法适应不同任务的需求，因此需要一种能够自动学习和优化通信结构的方法。

Method: 提出了QueenBee Planner框架，冻结工作智能体池、任务适配器和评分函数，仅训练外部LLM规划器来生成时序通信有向无环图（DAG），明确指定信息传递的方向、轮次、合并节点和输出节点。通过六种机制（保留验证集接受门控、方差感知信用分配、模体级归因、迁移信任、洞察证伪和结构去重）将执行轨迹提炼为具有三种动作（保留、修改、避免）的证据支持设计规则，防止自演化过程中的虚假模式。

Result: 在Count-Frequency聚合任务的全测试设置中，最优生成的图将RMSE从最强固定拓扑的12.53降低至7.87，同时减少了消息数量、模型调用次数和token成本；在Silo-Bench风格的分布式协调任务上也显示出优于冷启动生成和固定拓扑基线的改进。学习到的通信结构在固定工作智能体的条件下实现了持续的性能提升。

Conclusion: 多智能体系统可以学习可复用的架构设计知识，而非仅仅记忆任务答案，证明了将通信拓扑作为可学习设计技能的可行性和有效性，为多智能体系统的自动化架构设计开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) multi-agent systems increasingly depend not only on how individual agents reason, but also on how agents are connected. This paper introduces QueenBee Planner, a framework that treats inter-agent communication topology as a retrievable and self-improving design skill. A pool of worker agents, the task adapter, and the scoring function are frozen; only an outer LLM planner learns to generate temporal communication DAGs specifying who sends information to whom, in which round, who merges messages, and who emits the final answer. Execution traces are distilled into evidence-backed design rules with three actions: \emph{Preserve}, \emph{Modify}, and \emph{Avoid}. To prevent self-evolution from turning lucky runs or plausible but false explanations into policy, QueenBee uses held-out acceptance gates, variance-aware credit, motif-level attribution, transfer trust, insight falsification, and structural deduplication. We evaluate the method on Count-Frequency aggregation and Silo-Bench-style distributed coordination tasks. With fixed workers, self-evolved graph generation produces communication structures that improve over fixed topologies and cold generation. In the CF fulltest setting, the best generated graph reduces RMSE from 12.53 for the strongest fixed topology to 7.87 while also reducing messages, model calls, and token cost; Silo-style results show the same direction of improvement over cold and fixed-topology baselines. These results suggest that multi-agent systems can learn reusable architectural design knowledge rather than merely memorizing task answers.

</details>


### [25] [GenWorld: Empirically Grounded Urban Simulation Infrastructure for Scalable LLM-Agent Studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27650)
*Gen Li,Jieyuan Lan,Pengcheng Xu,Zongyuan Wu,Masaki Ogura,Tao Feng*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出了GenWorld，一个可扩展且基于现实数据的城市仿真框架，通过离线编译LLM决策信号解决大规模LLM智能体仿真的计算成本问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体仿真面临联合接地与扩展难题：一方面需要让智能体在反映真实城市约束的环境中行动，另一方面对城市规模人口进行在线LLM调用的计算成本过高。

Method: 构建GenWorld城市仿真基础设施，包含建筑级合成城市、结构化智能体-环境接口，以及将LLM推导的决策信号离线编译为查找策略以实现可扩展推演。以东广岛市为参考实例，将196,608名合成居民基于人口普查和地理空间数据进行建模。

Result: 在东广岛市实例中，GenWorld成功将合成居民基于人口普查和地理空间数据进行建模，通过人口普查数据验证人口一致性，并使用YJMob100K手机数据作为通勤距离诊断。通过三个可复现案例展示了该基础设施：全城工作日推演、工作日-周末行为对比，以及具有可审计重规划轨迹的预警-响应扰动。

Conclusion: GenWorld为接地且可扩展的LLM智能体研究提供了可复现的平台，但对交通、疏散或政策结果的校准预测仍是未来工作。

Abstract: LLM-agent simulation faces a joint grounding and scaling problem: agents should act in environments that reflect real urban constraints, yet direct online LLM calls for city-scale populations are computationally prohibitive. We present GenWorld, an empirically grounded urban simulation infrastructure that combines a building-level synthetic city, a structured agent-environment interface, and offline compilation of LLM-derived decision signals into lookup policies for scalable rollout. In a reference instantiation for Higashihiroshima, Japan, GenWorld grounds 196,608 synthetic residents in census and geospatial data, validates demographic consistency against census tabulations, and uses YJMob100K mobile-phone data as a commuting-distance diagnostic. We demonstrate the infrastructure through three reproducible cases: a full-city weekday rollout, a weekday-weekend behavioral contrast, and a warning-response perturbation with auditable replanning traces. These cases support GenWorld as a reproducible platform for grounded and scalable LLM-agent studies, while calibrated forecasting for traffic, evacuation, or policy outcomes remains future work.

</details>


### [26] [GBC: Gradient-Based Connections for Optimizing Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28187)
*Xiaocheng Yang,Abdulrahman Alrabah,Dilek Hakkani-Tür,Gokhan Tur*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出GBC方法，通过梯度反向传播实现多智能体系统的细粒度归因与优化，显著提升任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的多智能体系统存在协调失误问题，根本原因在于缺乏细粒度的信用分配机制。现有方法依赖粗粒度反馈，难以精准定位错误的智能体或交互步骤，限制了系统性能的进一步提升。

Method: 提出基于梯度的连接方法（GBC）：1）将多智能体系统建模为计算图；2）引入基于梯度的连接权重，在token级别量化各智能体输出对下游智能体的影响；3）构建归因图并反向传播任务特定损失信号；4）开发AgentChord高效实现，利用前缀梯度计算进行针对性提示优化。

Result: 在MultiWOZ和τ-bench数据集上的实验表明：GBC有效提升了多智能体系统性能，优于强单智能体和多智能体基线方法；归因质量越高，优化效果越好。

Conclusion: GBC通过细粒度归因实现了多智能体系统的精准错误定位和优化，为复杂任务的多智能体协作提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems (MAS) built on large language models (LLMs) provide a promising framework for solving complex tasks through role specialization and structured interaction. However, their performance is often limited by miscoordination and, more fundamentally, the lack of fine-grained credit assignment across agents. Existing approaches typically rely on coarse-grained feedback, making it difficult to identify which agents or interaction steps are responsible for errors. We propose Gradient-Based Connections (GBC), an approach for fine-grained attribution and optimization of multi-agent systems. GBC models a MAS as a computational graph and introduces gradient-based connection weights to quantify the influence of each agent's output on downstream agents at the token level. By constructing an attribution graph and propagating task-specific loss signals backward, our method enables precise identification of error sources and targeted prompt optimization. We further develop AgentChord, an efficient implementation that leverages prefix-based gradient computation. Experiments on MultiWOZ and τ-bench show that GBC improves multi-agent performance and outperforms strong single-agent and multi-agent baselines, and higher attribution quality is associated with greater optimization effectiveness. Code is available at: https://github.com/yxc-cyber/AgentChord.

</details>

### [27] [MultModLM: A multi-modal benchmark for Large-Language Model based hardware schematic generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27666)
*Dhruv Kulkarni,Sai Manoj Pudukotai Dinkarrao*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了MultModLM基准，用于评估多模态大语言模型从RTL代码生成硬件原理图的能力，发现现有模型虽能生成可视化原理图但功能正确性受限，且LLM评估器与人类评估者一致性接近零，揭示了在结构精确领域LLM评估范式的不可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM与硬件生成交叉研究主要关注文本任务，缺乏针对RTL设计的多模态LLM基准。同时，原理图表示的非唯一性给评估带来挑战，需要建立系统化的评估框架。

Method: 构建包含99个涵盖算术、控制、状态设计的RTL模块数据集MultModLM；提出多阶段评估框架，结合评分标准评估、自评估、跨模型评估、盲评和人工验证，以解决非唯一原理图表示的评估难题。

Result: 实验表明：SOTA模型能生成视觉可解释的原理图但功能正确性受限；LLM评估器与人类评估者一致性接近零，证明在结构精确领域LLM-as-a-judge范式不可靠。

Conclusion: 多模态硬件输出的可靠评估仍是开放挑战，需要开发更鲁棒的领域感知评估方法和结构评估工具，以支持形式等价性检查。

Abstract: Recently, Large Language models (LLMs) find application in several fields. This extends to hardware definition and synthesis. However, most works at the intersection of LLMs and hardware generation focus on text-based tasks, creating a gap for multi-modal LLMs for RTL design. In this work, we introduce MultModLM, a benchmark for evaluating LLMs on the task of generating hardware schematics from RTL (Register Transfer Level) descriptions. The dataset consists of 99 diverse RTL modules spanning arithmetic, control, and state-based designs. To address the challenges of non-unique schematic representations, we propose a multi-stage evaluation framework combining rubric-based scoring, self-evaluation, cross-model assessment, blind evaluation, and human validation to enable exhaustive evaluation.
  Through experiments on state-of-the-art LLMs, we observe that while models can generate visually interpretable schematics, their functional correctness remains constrained. Furthermore, we find that LLM-based evaluators exhibit near-zero agreement with human raters, revealing, as a key finding, that LLM-as-a-judge paradigms are unreliable in structurally precise domains. These findings suggest that reliable evaluation of multi-modal hardware outputs remains an open challenge, motivating the need for more robust and domain-aware evaluation methodologies, as well as tools for structural evaluation, so as to enable formal equivalence checkers.

</details>


### [28] [SEADA: An efficient methodology for optimizing mixed-precision DNNs on multi-precision spatial architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27884)
*Leandro Fiorin,Marco Ronzani,Cristina Silvano*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出了SEADA方法论，用于解决混合精度神经网络在多精度空间加速器上的高效映射问题，包含系统级成本模型、快速映射工具和基于比特级熵的精度选择方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合精度计算能有效降低DNN的延迟、能耗和内存占用，但在多精度空间架构上映射混合精度网络面临挑战：确定每层精度、平衡量化敏感性与架构约束、准确估计异构精度分配的系统成本。

Method: SEADA包含四个核心组件：(i)可配置的多精度空间加速器架构系统级分析成本模型；(ii)快速映射工具，识别DNN工作负载在目标整数加速器上的近最优映射；(iii)浮点层分析模型，估计混合精度执行的总体收益；(iv)基于比特级熵的逐层精度选择方法，实现跨多种数值精度的高效分配。

Result: SEADA为设计者提供了多精度架构设计空间探索的稳健框架，能够高效地解决混合精度映射中的精度确定、约束平衡和成本估计等挑战。

Conclusion: SEADA方法论的效率使其成为多精度架构设计空间探索的有效工具，为混合精度神经网络在空间加速器上的部署提供了系统化的解决方案。

Abstract: Mixed-precision computation has been introduced in deep neural networks (DNNs) as an effective approach to reduce latency, energy consumption, and memory footprint. However, efficiently mapping mixed-precision networks onto multi-precision spatial architectures poses several challenges. These include determining the appropriate precision for each layer, balancing layer-wise accuracy sensitivity to quantization against architectural heterogeneity and system-level constraints, and accurately estimating the system-level cost of heterogeneous precision assignments. This work presents SEADA, an efficient methodology designed to address these challenges. SEADA comprises: (i) a configurable system-level analytical cost model of a multi-precision spatial accelerator architecture; (ii) a fast mapping tool that identifies near-optimal mappings of DNN workloads onto the target integer accelerator; (iii) analytical models for floating-point layers to estimate the overall benefits of mixed-precision execution; and (iv) a per-layer precision selection methodology based on bit-level entropy, enabling efficient assignment across multiple numerical precisions. SEADA's efficiency provides designers with a robust framework for the design-space exploration of multi-precision architectures.

</details>


### [29] [Co-Optimization of Analog Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks for Low-Power Function Approximation in Flexible Electronics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27892)
*Paula Carolina Lozano Duarte,Georgios Zervakis,Mehdi Tahoori,Sani Nassif*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出模拟Kolmogorov-Arnold网络(AKANs)，通过软硬件协同优化实现柔性电子中的低功耗模拟函数近似，在节省30%平均面积和功耗的同时提升近似精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴设备和IoT传感器需要在传感器端处理生物信号和环境数据，涉及神经网络非线性激活函数、传感器校准曲线和信号预处理等计算密集型操作。这些函数通常包含超越运算和多变量依赖，数字实现成本高昂。柔性电子平台虽然适合可穿戴应用，但面临电路密度和功耗的严格约束，亟需高效的模拟实现方案。

Method: 提出模拟Kolmogorov-Arnold网络(AKANs)，采用软硬件协同优化方法。在训练过程中引入电路级误差建模以应对硬件缺陷，并在软件和硬件两个层面应用剪枝技术以减少面积和功耗。通过正则化样条参数，剪枝方法同时实现了硬件成本降低和近似精度提升。

Result: 在多个基准测试中验证，该方法实现高达55%的面积节省和50%的功耗节省，跨数据集平均减少近30%。剪枝方法不仅降低硬件成本，还能通过正则化样条参数提升近似精度，证明了AKANs在柔性电子低功耗模拟函数近似中的有效性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: AKANs为柔性电子应用提供了一个鲁棒且通用的低功耗模拟函数近似框架，成功解决了硬件缺陷下的复杂多变量函数近似问题，为可穿戴设备和IoT传感器的片上信号处理提供了高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Wearable devices and Internet of Things (IoT) sensors require on-sensor processing of biosignals and environmental data, including computationally demanding operations such as nonlinear activation functions for neural network inference, sensor calibration curves to map raw readings to physical units, and signal preprocessing functions like logarithmic compression and power operations for feature extraction. These functions exhibit significant complexity, often involving transcendental operations and multivariate dependencies that are costly to implement digitally. Analog function approximation provides a power-efficient alternative by performing these computations in the analog domain, thereby reducing the energy overhead associated with analog-to-digital conversion and subsequent digital processing. Flexible Electronics (FE) present a particularly attractive platform for wearable applications due to mechanical flexibility and low-cost fabrication, but impose strict constraints on circuit density and power consumption, making efficient analog implementations critical but challenging. This work introduces Analog Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (AKANs), developed via hardware-software co-optimization, to approximate these complex multivariate functions accurately under hardware imperfections. Our method incorporates circuit-level error modeling during training and applies pruning at both software and hardware levels to reduce area and power. Validation across multiple benchmarks demonstrates that our proposed pruning methodology not only reduces hardware cost but can also improve approximation accuracy by regularizing spline parameters. Results show up to 55% area and 50% power savings, with average reductions of nearly 30% across datasets, highlighting AKANs as a robust and generalizable framework for low-power analog function approximation in FE.

</details>


### [30] [Phase Matters: Characterizing Heterogeneous Vision-Language Inference on a Mobile SoC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27906)
*Aryama V Murthy,Yashas N Kotre,Prathmesh Sharma,Pragya Mishra,Sanjith Ganapathi,Priyesh Shukla*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文对移动端SoC上的视觉语言模型推理进行了全面特性分析，发现NPU加速效果因推理阶段而异（prefill 1.64x，decode仅1.18x），视觉编码器可获得20-45x加速；同时提出了四步图重写方法，使不支持的编码器获得高达22x的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管现代移动SoC提供了NPU执行路径用于设备端视觉语言模型推理，但开发者仍缺乏在异构后端上映射VLM流水线的阶段级指导，阻碍了移动端VLM的高效部署。

Method: 在Qualcomm SM8750（Snapdragon 8 Elite）平台上进行硬件在环特性分析，涵盖阶段吞吐量、缓存状态效应、100次运行的热稳定性、能耗、异构CPU/NPU流水线配置以及视觉token预算敏感性测试；以FastVLM-0.5B为端到端案例，结合四种架构系列的纯编码器测量；并提出四步图重写方法实现编码器移植。

Result: NPU执行高度依赖阶段：prefill阶段实现1.64x加速，decode阶段仅1.18x；视觉编码器相比CPU获得20-45x加速；这些增益带来稳态温度降低10.47°C，能耗降低2.52倍，避免了持续运行场景的热节流；四步图重写使Phi-3.5-V等编码器达到QNN路径，获得高达22x加速。

Conclusion: 阶段级别的优化对移动端VLM部署至关重要，NPU加速效果因推理阶段不同而差异显著；通过合理的流水线配置和图重写技术，可以显著提升移动端VLM推理性能、降低能耗并改善热稳定性。

Abstract: Recent phone-class mobile SoCs expose practical NPU execution paths for on-device vision-language model (VLM) inference, but developers still lack phase-level guidance for mapping VLM pipelines across heterogeneous backends. We present a hardware-in-the-loop characterization of VLM inference on the Qualcomm SM8750 (Snapdragon 8 Elite), covering phase throughput, cache-state effects, 100-run thermal stability, energy, heterogeneous CPU/NPU pipeline configurations, and visual-token-budget sensitivity. Using FastVLM-0.5B as an end-to-end case study, together with encoder-only measurements across four architecture families, we show that phase matters: NPU execution is highly phase-dependent, delivering 1.64x speedup for prefill but only 1.18x for decode, while vision encoders achieve 20-45x speedups over CPU. These gains translate into 10.47 degrees C lower steady-state temperature and 2.52x lower energy, avoiding thermal throttling in always-on settings. Finally, we show that a four-step graph rewrite enables previously unsupported encoders, such as Phi-3.5-V, to reach the QNN path with up to 22x speedup, providing a practical porting recipe for mobile VLM deployment.

</details>


### [31] [Agentic Hardware Design as Repository-Level Code Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28279)
*Cunxi Yu,Chenhui Deng,Nathaniel Pinckney,Brucek Khailany*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出HORIZON框架，将硬件设计视为仓库级代码演化，在四个基准测试上实现100%完成率，是首个将仓库级自演化从EDA软件系统扩展到硬件设计制品的工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作已实现EDA软件系统的仓库级自演化，但硬件设计制品本身的自动化演化尚未被探索。硬件设计过程可视为代码演化问题，需要框架支持领域知识整合、执行评估与状态管理。

Method: HORIZON框架将Markdown harness编译为项目包（包含领域知识、可执行评估器、接受谓词和git/运行时策略），通过免人工代理循环在隔离的git worktree中演化，利用仓库操作进行状态管理、追踪和回放。

Result: 在ChipBench、RTLLM、Verilog-Eval和9个CVDP类别共四个基准测试套件上实现100%基准完成率，全程采用免人工代理循环。

Conclusion: 该框架成功将仓库级自演化扩展至硬件设计领域，但作者明确表示硬件设计的代理AI问题尚未解决，这些基准测试仅是芯片设计更广泛工程问题的受控代理，文中讨论了当前研究的局限性和开放研究挑战。

Abstract: We present HORIZON, a self-evolving agent framework that treats hardware design as repository-level code evolution. A Markdown harness is compiled into a project pack containing domain knowledge, an executable evaluator, an acceptance predicate, and a git/runtime policy; a hands-free agent loop then evolves an isolated git worktree, using repository operations for state management, tracing, and replay. This extends prior works of repository-scale self-evolution from EDA software systems, to hardware-design artifacts themselves. We evaluate our approach on ChipBench, RTLLM, Verilog-Eval, and nine CVDP categories, achieving 100\% benchmark completion across all suites with a fully hands-free agentic loop. However, we do not claim that agentic AI for hardware design is solved: these benchmarks are controlled proxies for a much broader engineering problem in chip design. Section~\ref{sec:discuss} examines the limitations of the current study and highlights open research challenges.

</details>
