### [32] [Toward AI-Native 6G Air Interface: A 3GPP Perspective on Protocol Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27466)
*Xingqin Lin*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出6G空口AI原生架构应以协议框架标准化为核心，避免规定具体模型细节，并以神经接收机辅助参考信号适配为案例进行说明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI将在6G空口设计中发挥重要作用，但实现真正的AI原生空接口不仅是将学习算法应用于单个无线功能，更面临架构层面的挑战：当AI影响用户设备与网络对无线行为的解释、预测和适应方式时，空接口需提供通用协议语义以协调不同供应商和部署环境中的智能协作。

Method: 从3GPP视角提出AI原生6G空口协议框架，主张标准化应通过定义AI功能配置、验证、激活、监控及安全回退的协议机制来保障互操作性，同时避免规定模型架构、训练方法或模型权重以保留实现自由度。以神经接收机辅助参考信号适配作为案例研究，具体展示这一架构转型。

Result: 提出了面向3GPP的AI原生6G空口协议框架，明确了标准化应聚焦协议机制而非具体AI实现，通过神经接收机案例验证了该框架的实际可行性。

Conclusion: 6G标准化应聚焦协议框架定义，在保障互操作性与实现自由度之间取得平衡，为AI原生空接口的跨厂商协同部署提供基础。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) is expected to play an important role in the sixth-generation (6G) air interface design, but making the air interface truly AI-native requires more than applying learning algorithms to individual radio functions. The deeper challenge is architectural: once AI influences how the user equipment and network interpret, predict, and adapt radio behavior, the air interface must provide common protocol semantics for coordinating such intelligence across vendors and deployments. This article presents a 3rd generation partnership project (3GPP) oriented perspective on the protocol framework for AI-native 6G air interface. We argue that standardization should preserve implementation freedom by avoiding prescription of model architectures, training methods, or model weights. Instead, 6G should define the protocol framework needed for interoperable AI operation, including how AI-enabled functions are configured, validated, activated, monitored, and safely reverted to conventional operation. Neural receiver assisted reference signal adaptation is used as a case study to concretely show this broader architectural shift.

</details>


### [33] [Host-Driven Flowlet Balancing with Segment Routing over IPv6](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27697)
*Ryo Nakamura,Hiroki Kano,Tomoko Okuzawa*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于SRv6的完全主机驱动flowlet负载均衡方法，无需交换机维护每流状态，显著降低了尾延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代数据中心网络中，负载均衡对于有效利用多路径至关重要。Flowlet均衡比ECMP提供更细粒度的流量分割，预期能实现更高性能。然而，由于交换机需要维护每流状态导致的可扩展性问题，实际部署flowlet均衡仍具有挑战性。

Method: 主机检测发出流量中的flowlet，使用SRv6将其引导至特定路径。交换机以无状态方式作为SRv6节点运行。每个主机尽可能均匀地将flowlet分配到各路径上。引入一个简单模型估算每条路径上的在途字节数作为负载均衡指标。在Linux上实现该方法并在支持SRv6的路由器测试平台上进行评估。

Result: 在固定大小流量下，该方法相比随机flowlet均衡和ECMP分别将尾延迟降低了15%和33%。结合动态调整的flowlet超时机制，在两种应用工作负载下也提升了性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的主机驱动SRv6 flowlet均衡方法成功解决了交换机状态维护的可扩展性问题，在不增加交换机状态负担的情况下实现了比传统ECMP和随机flowlet均衡更优的性能表现，为数据中心网络负载均衡提供了实用的解决方案。

Abstract: This paper proposes a fully host-driven method for flowlet balancing with Segment Routing over IPv6 (SRv6). In modern data center networks, load balancing plays a pivotal role in efficiently utilizing multiple paths. Flowlet balancing offers finer granularity in traffic splitting than ECMP and is therefore expected to achieve higher performance. However, deploying flowlet balancing in practice is still challenging due to the scalability issue of switches having to maintain per-flow state. In our approach, hosts detect flowlets in their outgoing traffic and steer them onto specific paths using SRv6. The switches behave as SRv6 nodes in a stateless manner. Each host distributes its flowlets as evenly as possible across paths. As a metric for this load balancing, we introduce a simple model that estimates in-flight bytes on each path. We implemented the proposed method on Linux and evaluated it on a testbed with an SRv6-capable router. The results show that, under fixed-size flows, the proposed method reduces tail latency by 15% and 33% compared with random flowlet balancing and ECMP, respectively. Furthermore, combining the method with dynamically adjusted flowlet timeouts also improves performance under two application workloads.

</details>


### [34] [AB-Sync: Attention-Based Slot-Level Clock Synchronization Method for UWB-TDOA Localization Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28087)
*Tianyi Lyu,Kefei Tian,Kangqiao Qin,Qingwen Liu,Mingqing Liu*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了AB-Sync，一种基于注意力机制的时隙级时钟同步方法，通过解决时钟同步粒度不匹配问题，在无额外开销情况下显著提升了UWB-TDOA定位系统的精度和稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UWB-TDOA定位网络需要纳秒级时钟同步精度，现有无线时钟同步方法仅在同步阶段或间隔级别估计时钟状态，而TDMA系统的标签定位发生在同步阶段内的离散短时隙中，这种粒度不匹配导致了残余TDOA误差，影响定位精度。

Method: 提出AB-Sync方法，利用注意力机制建模目标标签闪烁所需时隙特定时钟速率比与邻近时钟波动观测之间的关系，实现时隙级时间戳映射，无需增加额外的UWB同步消息开销。

Result: 在真实UWB-TDOA测试平台上，相比Deferred+3S-KF基准方法，AB-Sync将多锚节点平均TDOA测距标准差降低9.4%，静态定位精度提升18.6%；在五时隙多标签实验中，平均降低STD.V 5.3%，单时隙最高降低16.2%。

Conclusion: AB-Sync成功解决了时钟同步粒度不匹配问题，在不增加额外同步开销的前提下，有效提升了UWB-TDOA系统的定位精度和稳定性，验证了时隙级时钟同步的可行性和优越性。

Abstract: Ultra-wideband (UWB) time-difference-of-arrival (TDOA) localization networks provide high-update-rate indoor location services for IoT and cyber-physical applications, but their accuracy depends on nanosecond-level clock synchronization among anchors. Existing wireless clock synchronization (WCS) methods typically estimate clock states at the synchronization-stage or interval level, whereas TDMA-based UWB-TDOA systems localize tags from blinks transmitted in discrete short slots inside each synchronization stage. We identify this granularity mismatch as a source of residual TDOA error and present AB-Sync, an attention-based slot-level clock synchronization method. AB-Sync models the relationship between the slot-specific clock-speed ratio required by a target tag blink and neighboring clock-fluctuation observations, thereby enabling tag-slot-level timestamp mapping without adding extra UWB synchronization messages. On a real UWB-TDOA testbed, AB-Sync reduces the multi-anchor average TDOA ranging STD.V by 9.4% and improves representative static localization accuracy by 18.6% compared with Deferred+3S-KF, the leading low-overhead baseline in our evaluation. In a five-slot multi-tag experiment, AB-Sync consistently improves localization stability across all TDMA slots, reducing STD.V by 5.3% on average and up to 16.2% per slot with no extra UWB synchronization overhead.

</details>


### [35] [V-TSN: A Software-Defined TSN Overlay for General-Purpose Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28285)
*Mohammadparsa Karimi,Majid Nabi,Ahmed Khalaf,Andrew Nelson,Kees Goossens,Twan Basten*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了V-TSN，一种软件定义的覆盖网络方案，可在通用非确定性网络上实现类TSN功能，无需专用硬件支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间敏感网络（TSN）通常需要专用硬件来实现确定性通信，但在设计和验证阶段，离线仿真无法以实时速度运行应用软件；在部署阶段，许多通用或云网络缺乏原生TSN支持，配置完整TSN硬件成本高昂或不切实际。

Method: 提出虚拟时间敏感网络（V-TSN），这是一种软件定义的覆盖网络，在通用非确定性网络上实现基于gPTP的时钟同步和TSN流量整形。V-TSN实时运行于未修改的应用协议栈旁，通过虚拟时间感知整形器（TAS）隔离时间关键流量，通过虚拟基于信用的整形器（CBS）执行每类带宽预留。

Result: 在云部署环境中，V-TSN实现了平均时钟偏移低于200微秒的同步精度，成功通过虚拟TAS隔离时间关键流量，并通过虚拟CBS执行了每类带宽预留。

Conclusion: V-TSN可作为开发阶段的仿真工具和时序要求放宽场景下的低成本部署方案，有效弥补了专用TSN硬件不可用时的应用需求。

Abstract: Time-Sensitive Networking (TSN) extends Ethernet with deterministic communication for time-critical applications such as industrial automation, in-vehicle networks, and cyber-physical systems. However, realizing TSN behavior without dedicated hardware is difficult. During design and validation, offline simulation cannot run application software at real-time speed when costly specialized TSN hardware is not (yet) available. At deployment time, many systems run on general-purpose and cloud networks with no native TSN support, where provisioning full TSN hardware is unnecessary or impractical for applications that tolerate relaxed timing. In this paper, we introduce Virtual Time-Sensitive Networking (V-TSN), a software-defined overlay that realizes gPTP-based synchronization and TSN traffic shaping over general-purpose, non-deterministic networks without specialized hardware. V-TSN runs in real time alongside the unmodified application stack, serving both as a development-time emulation tool and as a cost-efficient deployment option where relaxed timing is acceptable. In a cloud-based deployment, V-TSN achieves an average clock offset below 200 microseconds, it isolates time-critical traffic through a virtual Time-Aware Shaper (TAS), and it enforces per-class bandwidth reservations through a virtual Credit-Based Shaper (CBS).

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [36] [How far does a random forest generalize from a 54-run LAMMPS+SPICA benchmark?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27695)
*Dennis Alves Pedersen,Paulo Henrique Leme Ramalho,Fábio Andrijauskas*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种冷启动随机森林代理模型，仅通过54次实验训练即可预测分子动力学工作负载的MPI+OpenMP配置性能，避免昂贵的穷举基准测试。模型在单一硬件体制内能准确推荐高性能配置，但跨架构边界时性能下降，为后续基准测试活动提供了可信的范围指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上，为现代HPC集群上的分子动力学工作负载选择近最优的混合MPI+OpenMP配置需要进行详尽的实证基准测试，其消耗的计算资源与评估的配置数量成正比。这种穷举式方法成本高昂，因此需要一种能够以少量训练数据准确预测性能并推荐高性能配置的方法，从而显著减少基准测试所需的集群资源分配。

Method: 研究采用冷启动随机森林代理模型，在Lovelace集群上使用包含54个LAMMPS+SPICA运行样本的结构化基准数据集进行一次性训练。数据集涵盖抗微生物肽Tritrpticin在含水DOPC双层膜上的模拟，跨越18种混合配置（1-8个AMD EPYC 7662节点），每种配置进行三次独立重复实验。使用9个拓扑和资源特征输入到五个回归器中，分别预测循环时间和四个内部LAMMPS时间分数。通过特征重要性分析和留一维度交叉验证来评估模型的泛化能力。

Result: 模型在样本内循环时间预测的平均绝对误差为0.49秒（相对误差4.0%）。特征重要性分析表明，预测信号主要来自拓扑变量（OpenMP线程数和MPI/OpenMP比率占主导地位，而原始节点和核心计数贡献不足3%）。留一维度泛化分析揭示了准确性的关键因素：在共同的硬件体制内（单节点、多节点或共享线程层级），代理模型能够正确排序配置性能；但当目标跨越架构边界时，模型性能退化。研究产出了一个可解释的映射，标明了代理模型推荐的信任边界。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了一个小规模训练的随机森林代理模型可以在特定硬件体制内可靠地预测分子动力学工作负载的配置性能并推荐近最优配置。研究结果提供了一个可解释的信任边界图，明确了模型推荐有效的范围，这对于规划后续基准测试活动具有重要价值，能够以名义成本的一小部分实现配置选择的优化。这种方法为HPC应用性能调优提供了一种经济高效的替代方案。

Abstract: Selecting near-optimal hybrid MPI+OpenMP configurations for molecular dynamics workloads on modern HPC clusters has traditionally required exhaustive empirical benchmarking, consuming allocation budget proportional to the number of configurations evaluated. This work investigates whether a cold-start Random Forest surrogate, trained once on a small, structured benchmark dataset, can reliably predict execution performance and recommend high-performing configurations without further cluster runs. The training dataset comprises 54 LAMMPS+SPICA runs of the antimicrobial peptide Tritrpticin on a hydrated DOPC bilayer (4 354 coarse-grained beads), spanning 18 hybrid configurations on 1-8 AMD EPYC 7662 nodes of the Lovelace cluster at CENAPAD-SP, with three independent replications each. Nine topology and resource features feed five regressors that predict loop time and four internal LAMMPS timing fractions (Pair, Kspace, Comm, Modify). In-sample mean absolute error is 0.49 s on loop time (4.0 % relative). Feature importance localizes predictive signal in topology variables (OpenMP threads and MPI/OpenMP ratio dominate; raw node and core counts contribute under 3 %). Leave-one-dimension-out generalization reveals that accuracy is governed by hardware regime membership: within a common regime (single-node, multi-node, or shared threading tier) the surrogate ranks configurations correctly, and degrades when targets cross architectural boundaries. The result is an interpretable map of where the surrogate's recommendations can be trusted, useful for scoping further benchmark campaigns at a fraction of their nominal cost.

</details>


### [37] [Lightweight Multi-Vehicle Collaborative Perception Acceleration with Fusion Position Adjustment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27750)
*Wenzhao Zhang,Shujun Han,Haixiao Gao,Mengying Sun,Bizhu Wang,Xiaodong Xu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了FALL方案，通过在神经网络线性层间调整特征融合位置，实现了多车协同感知在有限资源下的无精度损失加速，最高可降低74.8%的通信延迟和30.3%的计算延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多车协同感知是自动驾驶的关键技术，但在实际应用中面临计算和通信资源受限的挑战。如何在保证感知精度的前提下，实现资源受限环境下的实时MvCP，是亟待解决的问题。

Method: 提出了条件可加性的松弛定义，分析了各类DNN线性层的条件可加性；基于此推导了特征融合位置调整在前后向融合中的精度一致性；设计了FALL方案，通过在线性层间调整融合位置来加速MvCP。

Result: 实验验证了融合位置调整方法的精度一致性；仿真结果表明FALL方案在有限通信资源下可降低高达74.8%的MvCP延迟，在有限计算资源下可降低高达30.3%的延迟。

Conclusion: 所提出的FALL方案能够在理论上保证无精度损失的前提下，有效解决资源受限场景下MvCP的加速问题，为自动驾驶提供了实用的轻量化协同感知解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-vehicle collaborative perception (MvCP) is considered as a key technology to facilitate automated driving (AD), where real-time MvCP under limited resources is significant for reliable AD. In this paper, we formulate a lightweight acceleration scheme for intermediate-fusion (IF) MvCP, which can adapt to both situations of limited computation and communication resources. We provide a relaxed definition conditional additivity and analyze the conditional additivity for various DNN linear layers. On this basis, we focus on the IF-MvCP based on additive feature fusion, and derive the MvCP precision consistency of the forward and backward feature fusion position (FP) adjustments among linear layers. Through experiments, we further validate the precision consistency of the FP adjustment method. Moreover, we propose an FP adjustment among linear layers (FALL) scheme for MvCP acceleration without precision loss theoretically. Simulation results show that the proposed FALL can reduce MvCP latency by up to 74.8% under limited communication resources and by up to 30.3% under limited computation resources.

</details>


### [38] [Optimizing Teacher-Student Partitioning for Scalable Knowledge Distillation on HPC Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27797)
*Adrian P. Dieguez,Victor Conchello Vendrell,Alex Batlle,Vinnam Kim,Jordi Ros-Giralt,Harris Teague*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出面向高性能计算的知识蒸馏方法，通过解耦教师-学生模型分区策略，相比TRL库实现高达67%的吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TRL库在实现知识蒸馏时对称处理教师和学生模型，忽略了两者在内存占用和通信需求方面的显著不对称性，错失了优化机会。

Method: 提出HPC感知的知识蒸馏方法：1）解耦教师和学生模型的分区策略；2）避免不必要的教师模型数据结构；3）结合垂直和水平模型分区；4）推导解析表达式识别分区策略间的拐点。

Result: 在生产HPC集群上相比TRL库实现高达67%的样本处理速度提升，显著加速了GKD训练过程。

Conclusion: 通过拓扑感知的并行策略充分利用教师-学生模型的不对称性，可以有效提升知识蒸馏在HPC环境下的训练效率。

Abstract: Knowledge Distillation (KD) enables training smaller student models under the guidance of larger teacher models, and the widely adopted TRL library implements it. Yet, TRL treats both models symmetrically, missing opportunities to exploit their pronounced asymmetry in memory footprint, and communication requirements. This paper presents an HPC-aware methodology for KD that decouples teacher and student partitioning efficiently. Our approach achieves up to 67% higher samples-per-second than TRL by avoiding unnecessary teacher-model data structures and selecting the best split strategy. We combine vertical and horizontal partitioning of models, deriving an analytical expression that identifies the existence of inflection points between splitting regimes. These results showed that exploiting teacher--student asymmetry through topology-aware parallelism notably accelerated GKD training on production HPC clusters at our company

</details>


### [39] [RAMSES: Secure high-performance computing for sensitive data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27919)
*Peter Heger,Lech Nieroda,Roland Pabel,Christoph Stollwerk,Stefan Borowski,Kamil Tokmakov,Michael Commer,Martin Peifer,Stefan Wesner,Viktor Achter*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了RAMSES，一个兼顾高性能与高安全性的HPC系统，通过硬件内存加密、文件加密及多层安全架构实现全生命周期数据保护，且性能损失有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统HPC系统追求速度而牺牲安全性，而生命科学等领域在处理敏感大规模数据时需要兼顾两者，因此有必要设计一个能同时满足高性能计算与严格安全合规要求的系统。

Method: 集成AMD处理器的硬件内存加密、IBM Storage Scale文件加密与Thales CipherTrust管理器，实施操作系统加固、多层安全架构与强制多因素认证，构建覆盖数据全生命周期的加密保护体系，符合GDPR、ISO/IEC 27001及FIPS等标准。

Result: 生物医学领域的基准测试表明，该安全环境带来的性能影响有限，成功整合了原本冲突的速度与安全需求，同时保持了系统的连贯性、灵活性与用户友好性。

Conclusion: 证明了在高性能计算环境中实现高水平安全保护的可行性，为需要处理敏感数据的科学领域提供了一个性能与安全兼顾的解决方案范例。

Abstract: Traditionally, the architecture of high-performance computing (HPC) systems is tailored for speed, while highly secure computer systems must sacrifice speed for security. However, a wide range of scientific domains, such as the life sciences, call for a combination of performance and security to allow processing sensitive data at scale. Here, we present RAMSES (Research Accelerator for Modeling and Simulation with Enhanced Security), an HPC system designed from the ground up to deliver high performance within a robust security framework. RAMSES integrates hardware-based memory encryption of AMD processors with state-of-the-art file encryption from IBM Storage Scale and the Thales CipherTrust manager, establishing an HPC platform that ensures continuous encryption throughout the data life cycle - at rest, in transit, and in use - in compliance with major data protection standards (European General Data Protection Regulation, ISO/IEC 27001 certification, and Federal Information Processing Standards). In addition, we implemented advanced operating system hardening, a multi-layered security architecture, and mandatory multi-factor authentication to adapt the HPC environment to increased security demands. Benchmark results from the biomedical sector demonstrate that the performance impact of the secure environment is limited and that integration of the conflicting requirements speed and security can be achieved while preserving a coherent, flexible, and user-friendly system.

</details>


### [40] [DataStates-LLM: Scalable Checkpointing for Transformer Models Using Composable State Providers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.16956)
*Avinash Maurya,M. Mustafa Rafique,Franck Cappello,Bogdan Nicolae*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出DataStates-LLM，一种针对大规模LLM训练的新型检查点架构，通过State Providers抽象和异步非阻塞快照，在70B参数规模上实现4倍检查点吞吐量提升和2.2倍训练时间缩短。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型Transformer模型参数规模已达万亿级，需在数千GPU上采用混合并行策略训练，检查点对容错、暂停恢复、模型分析等至关重要。现有方案将模型状态视为不透明二进制块，忽视了"3D异构性"（内存位置、分片对象数量、数据类型、序列化需求差异），导致设备到主机阻塞传输、数据盲序列化和存储I/O争用等显著运行时开销。

Method: 提出DataStates-LLM架构：1）引入State Providers解耦状态抽象与数据移动；2）利用前向和反向传播中模型参数的不可变性执行"惰性"非阻塞异步快照；3）通过State Providers高效合并碎片化异构分片；4）重叠元数据序列化与大容量张量I/O操作。

Result: 在256张A100-40GB GPU上对70B参数模型评估，DataStates-LLM相比最先进方案实现：检查点吞吐量提升最高4倍，端到端训练时间减少最高2.2倍，有效缓解了大规模LLM训练中的序列化和异构性瓶颈。

Conclusion: DataStates-LLM通过State Providers架构创新性地解决了大规模分布式LLM训练中检查点的异构性和序列化瓶颈问题，在保持模型状态完整性的同时显著降低了性能开销，为极端规模模型训练提供了高效的检查点解决方案。

Abstract: The rapid growth of Large Transformer-based models, specifically Large Language Models (LLMs), now scaling to trillions of parameters, has necessitated training across thousands of GPUs using complex hybrid parallelism strategies (e.g., data, tensor, and pipeline parallelism). Checkpointing this massive, distributed state is critical for a wide range of use cases, such as resilience, suspend-resume, investigating undesirable training trajectories, and explaining model evolution. However, existing checkpointing solutions typically treat model state as opaque binary blobs, ignoring the ``3D heterogeneity'' of the underlying data structures--varying by memory location (GPU vs. Host), number of ``logical'' objects sharded and split across multiple files, data types (tensors vs. Python objects), and their serialization requirements. This results in significant runtime overheads due to blocking device-to-host transfers, data-oblivious serialization, and storage I/O contention. In this paper, we introduce DataStates-LLM, a novel checkpointing architecture that leverages State Providers to decouple state abstraction from data movement. DataStates-LLM exploits the immutability of model parameters during the forward and backward passes to perform ``lazy'', non-blocking asynchronous snapshots. By introducing State Providers, we efficiently coalesce fragmented, heterogeneous shards and overlap the serialization of metadata with bulk tensor I/O. We evaluate DataStates-LLM on models up to 70B parameters on 256 A100-40GB GPUs. Our results demonstrate that DataStates-LLM achieves up to 4$\times$ higher checkpointing throughput and reduces end-to-end training time by up to 2.2$\times$ compared to state-of-the-art solutions, effectively mitigating the serialization and heterogeneity bottlenecks in extreme-scale LLM training.

</details>

### [41] [OverFlowLight: Real-Time Gridlock Prevention and Traffic Signal Optimization for Urban Intersections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27381)
*Mingyuan Li,Boyang Huang,Tianqi Jiang,Chenpu Li,Chunyu Liu,Yang Li,Ruimin Li,Qiang Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了OverFlowLight，一个实时预防交通队列溢出的框架，在三个城市的43个路口实际部署后，溢出事件减少60.4%，网络吞吐量提升18.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交通信号控制算法主要优化吞吐量，难以应对高峰期的队列溢出问题，导致拥堵加剧并引发安全隐患。

Method: 利用摄像头和雷达的多模态感知实时检测溢出，动态生成并插入专用溢出相位清理阻塞队列，采用混合控制设计结合基于规则的快速干预与强化学习等控制器后端。

Result: 在三个城市的43个路口进行实际部署，与部署基线相比溢出事件减少60.4%，网络吞吐量提升18.2%，显著减少了人工干预需求。

Conclusion: 这是首个实用的、可扩展的、数据驱动的主动预防交通网格锁框架，可与现有强化学习交通信号控制代理无缝集成，为构建韧性高效的城市交通系统提供了关键组件。

Abstract: Queue overflow, a severe consequence of urban traffic congestion, occurs when vehicle queues exceed intersection capacity, obstructing upstream traffic and triggering cascading gridlocks. Prevailing traffic signal control (TSC) algorithms, primarily optimized for throughput, often fail to address overflow during peak hours, exacerbating congestion and creating safety hazards. We propose OverFlowLight, a real-time framework designed to preemptively resolve overflow and enhance overall TSC performance. It first introduces a mechanism to accurately detect overflow in real-time by leveraging multi-modal sensing from cameras and radars. Upon detection, it dynamically generates and inserts dedicated overflow phases into the signal cycle to clear the blocking queues. This is orchestrated by a hybrid control design that combines rapid rule-based overflow intervention with controller back ends such as reinforcement learning (RL) for longer-horizon efficiency. We conducted extensive real-world deployments of OverFlowLight across 43 intersections in three major cities. The framework demonstrates seamless integration with existing RL-based TSC agents, highlighting its modularity and practical applicability. Empirical results show that OverFlowLight reduces overflow incidents by 60.4% and increases network throughput by 18.2% compared to deployed baselines. Furthermore, it substantially diminishes the need for manual intervention common with expert-tuned signal plans. This work presents the first practical, scalable, and data-driven framework for actively preventing traffic gridlock, offering a crucial component for building resilient and efficient urban transportation systems. Our demonstration videos, codes and datasets are available at the anonymous URL, https://anonymous.4open.science/r/OverFlowLight-FBF9.

</details>


### [42] [RANSAC Scoring Done Right](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27385)
*James Pritts,Felix Seegräber,Kevin Köser*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需内点尺度参数的新型RANSAC评分方法，通过解析边缘化内点尺度，在阈值误校准和数据稀缺场景下显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RANSAC变体需要用户提供与内点尺度相关的参数，该参数需从受污染数据中估计，引入了参数敏感性。研究旨在消除这种依赖，实现无需预设尺度参数的模型评分。

Method: 采用逆向推理顺序：在内点划分固定、尺度共轭先验（Inverse-Gamma）下，解析地边缘化内点尺度，得到闭式评分表达式；该表达式统一非信息Jeffreys先验与信息经验贝叶斯先验，并通过排序扫描实现O(N log N)计算。

Result: 在近7万对图像基准上，所提评分在阈值误校准时保持近稳定而基线显著退化；仅需2对验证对即达近最优精度，而基线需约100倍数据量；随验证数据减少自动增强先验正则化。

Conclusion: 该方法是首个评分公式中真正不含内点尺度的RANSAC评分方法，自适应于数据丰富与稀缺场景，无需更改算法，为鲁棒估计提供了更强的参数鲁棒性与数据效率。

Abstract: The most widely used RANSAC variants score candidate models by counting inliers or summing per-point scores that saturate beyond a residual threshold. Every such score requires a user-supplied parameter that is a function of the inlier scale, which must itself be estimated from contaminated data. We remove this dependence by reversing the usual order of inference: rather than estimating the scale and then scoring against it, we marginalize the inlier scale analytically in closed form under a conjugate Inverse-Gamma prior for a fixed inlier partition, then optimize over partitions. A single closed-form expression spans the non-informative Jeffreys limit and informative empirical-Bayes priors, so the same score adapts across data-rich and data-scarce regimes without any change to the algorithm. The proposed RANSAC score is the first in which the inlier scale is genuinely absent from the formula. The score admits O(N log N ) computation via sort-and-sweep. On a benchmark of nearly 70 000 image pairs spanning different two-view estimation problems and both engineered and learned feature pipelines, the proposed score exceeds the state of the art (RANSAC, MSAC, GaU, MAGSAC): it stays nearly flat under threshold miscalibration where baselines degrade, reaches near-optimal accuracy from as few as two validation pairs where baselines need ont he order of 100 times more,. and tightens its prior regularization as validation data grows scarce.

</details>


### [43] [Unified Zero-Shot Time Series Forecasting: A Darts Foundation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27438)
*Zhihao Dai,Dennis Bader,Alain Gysi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Darts库集成了多种基础模型（Chronos-2、TimesFM 2.5、TiRex、PatchTST-FM），提供统一接口实现零样本预测、微调、不确定性估计和回测，解决了基础模型碎片化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型在零样本预测方面展现了潜力，但通常以孤立包的形式发布，接口碎片化且与通用工具互操作性有限，使得联合评估和在完整流程中的集成变得困难。

Method: 在Darts库中开发统一的$\texttt{FoundationModel}$类集合，包含Chronos-2、TimesFM 2.5、TiRex、PatchTST-FM等模型，提供标准化的全周期预测接口，且外部依赖最小化。

Result: 现有Darts流程仅需更改模型名称即可使用基础模型；新流程可在统一框架内使用基础模型进行零样本或微调预测、不确定性估计和回测，并结合数据处理和评估工具。

Conclusion: 统一的基础模型接口降低了集成门槛，使研究者和从业者能够便捷地在现有工作流程中利用前沿的基础模型能力，推动时序分析生态系统的整合发展。

Abstract: Since its initial release in 2020, Darts has become a widely used open-source Python library for time series analysis. A series of foundation models have recently claimed accuracy improvements in zero-shot forecasting, promising a paradigm shift from training custom models to harnessing pre-trained general-purpose forecasters. Foundation models, however, are often released as isolated packages with fragmented interfaces and limited interoperability with common tooling, making joint evaluation and integration within complete pipelines difficult. In Darts, we developed a unified $\texttt{FoundationModel}$ class collection (Chronos-2, TimesFM 2.5, TiRex, PatchTST-FM) that provides standardized, full-cycle forecasting interfaces with minimal external dependencies for integrating foundation models into the ecosystem. Existing Darts pipelines can now use foundation models with only a name change; new pipelines can use them for zero-shot or fine-tuned forecasting, uncertainty estimation, and backtesting, combined with data processing and evaluation tooling, all within a unified framework.

</details>


### [44] [PairSAE: Mechanistic Interpretability from Pair Representations in Protein Co-Folding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27440)
*Giosue Migliorini,Aristofanis Rontogiannis,Grigori Guitchounts,Nicholas Franklin,Axel Elaldi,Olivia Viessmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PairSAE方法，通过N-mode SVD将配对张量分解为token级交互角色，再用稀疏自编码器学习共享特征，成功解决了标准SAE在pairformer架构上的应用难题，并在蛋白质-配体复合物任务中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构生物学基础模型虽在预测生物分子结构方面表现优异，但理解其内部特征如何驱动输出仍具挑战。标准稀疏自编码器（SAE）在transformer序列嵌入上有效，却难以迁移到pairformer架构：直接操作配对表示会导致特征二次方爆炸，且模糊了跨序列和配对表示联合分布的概念。

Method: 提出PairSAE方法：1）使用N-mode SVD将成对张量总结为token级交互角色；2）利用稀疏自编码器学习共享的token级特征集；3）将这些特征解码到序列和配对表示中。该方法避免了传统SAE在pairformer架构上的二次方爆炸问题。

Result: 在Boltz-2激活数据上针对PLINDER蛋白质-配体复合物进行评估，PairSAE生成的可解释特征与UniProt注释相吻合，并能预测Boltz-2亲和力值。结果表明PairSAE成功将结构生物学基础模型的潜在空间与可解释的结构概念联系起来。

Conclusion: PairSAE成功解决了传统SAE在pairformer架构上的应用限制，为理解结构生物学基础模型的内部机制提供了有效工具，将模型的潜在表示与可解释的结构概念相连接，阐明了模型所"知道"的内容。

Abstract: Foundation models for structural biology have achieved remarkable performance in predicting biomolecular structure and show promise for the design of proteins and small molecules. Yet understanding which internal features drive their outputs remains challenging. Standard sparse autoencoders (SAEs), effective on transformer-style sequence embeddings, do not transfer cleanly to pairformer-like architectures: naively operating on pairwise representations yields a quadratic blow-up of features and obscures concepts distributed jointly across sequence and pair representations.
  We introduce PairSAE, which summarizes pairwise tensors via an N-mode SVD into token-wise interaction roles, then uses a sparse autoencoder to learn a shared set of token-level features that decode into both sequence and pair representations. Evaluated on Boltz-2 activations for PLINDER protein-ligand complexes, PairSAE yields interpretable features that align with UniProt annotations and predict Boltz-2 affinity values. These results indicate that PairSAE links the latent space of foundation models for structural biology to interpretable structural concepts, clarifying what the model "knows" while avoiding pairformer-induced pitfalls that limit conventional SAEs.

</details>


### [45] [FoggyTrust: Robust Federated Learning with Hierarchical Trust Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27622)
*Emmanuel Rassou,Tomas Gonzalez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FOGGYTRUST，一种层级化的拜占庭鲁棒联邦学习框架，通过将信任计算本地化到雾节点，有效处理全局异构数据，在CIFAR-10数据集上相比FLTrust提升超过50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的FLTrust方法使用服务器端的根数据集为客户端更新分配信任分数，但在处理全局异构数据时存在局限性。需要一种方法既能保持对恶意客户端的鲁棒性，又能更好地处理数据异构性和客户端漂移问题。

Method: 提出FOGGYTRUST层级框架，将信任计算从中央服务器下放到雾节点，实现局部信任聚合；结合局部基于信任的聚合与异构感知的全局优化器（如FedAdam和SCAFFOLD），形成两级架构，同时解决信任估计中的分布不匹配和跨组客户端漂移问题。

Result: 在基准数据集上，FOGGYTRUST在更具挑战性的异构设置中表现最佳，特别是在CIFAR-10数据集上对抗Krum和Trim攻击时，相比FLTrust提升超过50%。在真实世界的野生动物监测数据集上也验证了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 层级信任网络为分布式野生动物监测等社会影响重大、安全关键的联邦学习场景提供了有前景的解决方案，能够同时实现鲁棒性和异构数据处理能力的提升。

Abstract: Byzantine-robust federated learning seeks to protect distributed model training from malicious or corrupted clients without requiring access to their private data. FLTrust addresses this challenge by introducing a trusted server-side root dataset that assigns trust scores to client updates for more robust aggregation. In this work, we propose FOGGYTRUST, a hierarchical extension of FLTrust that localizes trust computation to fog nodes, allowing the framework to better handle globally heterogeneous data while preserving robustness within locally homogeneous client groups. We further show that this two-level architecture can simultaneously address distribution mismatch in trust estimation and client drift across groups by combining local trust-based aggregation with heterogeneity-aware global optimizers such as FedAdam and SCAFFOLD. Across benchmark datasets, FOGGYTRUST achieves its strongest gains on more challenging heterogeneous settings, particularly on CIFAR-10 under Krum and Trim attacks, where it achieves an over 50% improvement over FLTrust. We also test FOGGYTRUST in a real-world safari dataset to show the promise of hierarchical trust networks for robust federated learning in socially impactful, safety-critical settings such as distributed wildlife monitoring.

</details>


### [46] [Learning in Markovian bandits with non-observable states and constrained decision epochs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27448)
*Thomas Hira,Victor Boone,Urtzi Ayesta,Ina Maria Verloop*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究状态不可观测的马尔可夫老虎机在纯策略基准下的遗憾最小化问题，提出了自退化马尔可夫老虎机模型，设计了UCB-NOM算法实现近对数遗憾，并在已知偏差函数界的情况下达到对数级遗憾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究具有不可观测状态且决策时期可能受限的马尔可夫老虎机中的遗憾最小化问题。关注"纯"遗憾基准，即将学习算法的性能与始终选择同一臂的最优纯策略进行比较，类似于随机老虎机的最优策略。

Method: 引入自退化马尔可夫老虎机（self-degrading Markov bandits）这一广义休息型马尔可夫老虎机，在该模型中纯策略渐近最优。设计了UCB-NOM算法，这是一种受UCB启发的乐观算法。

Result: 证明了在不知道底层老虎机先验知识的情况下，很少切换臂的算法遗憾必然超对数增长。UCB-NOM算法实现近对数遗憾。给定偏差函数界的先验知识时，UCB-NOM达到O(log(T))遗憾，并具有O(√(T log(T))的最坏情况遗憾界。值得注意的是，遗憾界不依赖于底层马尔可夫链的状态数。

Conclusion: 状态不可观测性在自退化马尔可夫老虎机中只是一个轻微的不便。通过适当的先验知识，可以实现最优的对数级遗憾，表明该模型具有良好的可学习性。

Abstract: This paper studies the problem of regret minimization in Markovian bandits with \emph{non-observable states} and possibly \emph{constrained} decision epochs. The focus is restricted to a ``pure'' regret benchmark, that compares the performance of the learning algorithm to the best \emph{pure policy} which -- akin to optimal policies of stochastic bandits -- picks the optimal arm from start to finish without ever switching. We introduce a generalization of rested Markovian bandits, \emph{self-degrading Markovian bandits}, for which pure policies are always asymptotically optimal.We show that without prior knowledge on the underlying bandit, the regret of algorithms that switch arms rarely necessarily scales super-logarithmically for every bandit, i.e., as $ω(\log(T))$, where $T$ is the learning horizon. Despite the unreachability of the logarithmic regime, we design UCB-NOM, an optimistic algorithm inspired by UCB, of which the regret is nearly logarithmic. Lastly, we show that given prior knowledge on the Markovian bandit in the form of a bound on the bias functions of its arm, a proper instantiation of UCB-NOM achieves $O(\log(T))$ regret. We further show that this prior knowledge allows for a $O(\sqrt{T \log(T)})$ worst-case regret bound for UCB-NOM. Notably, our regret bounds do not depend on the number of states of the underlying Markov chains. Our findings suggest that the non-observability of states is a mild inconvenience in self-degrading Markovian bandits.

</details>


### [47] [Towards Value-Constrained Credit Assignment in Fully Delegated AI Cooperatives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28217)
*Young Yoon,Jimin Kim,Soyeon Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向全委托AI协作体的奖励分配框架，通过遍历学习（TL）实现价值条件下的梯度筛选、在线边际贡献信号和累计收益结算，为具有异质价值约束的参与者提供精准的贡献归因与奖励分配机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在全委托AI协作体中，人类由代理代表，这些代理贡献数据并参与模型更新，但存在异质的价值约束。现有方法如聚合中心式分布式学习和FedAvg式联邦学习存在质量损失，且无法提供精细的归因基础，难以准确评估和奖励各参与方的贡献。

Method: 核心方法包括三个组件：（1）价值条件梯度筛选——仅认可通过各委托方价值档案审查的更新；（2）在线边际贡献信号——实时计算各参与方的边际贡献；（3）累计收益结算——在遍历学习（TL）基质上实现去中心化反向传播。TL方法的优势在于避免了聚合中心式学习的质量损失，同时保留了显式的遍历和梯度路径，提供比FedAvg更精细的归因基础。

Result: 该框架在数据估值、联邦贡献估计、个性化联邦学习和多元对齐等应用场景中进行了定位分析，证明了其在全委托AI协作体中的适用性和优势，特别是在保持更新质量和提供精细贡献归因方面的表现。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架成功解决了全委托AI协作体中的奖励分配问题，通过遍历学习基质实现了在异质价值约束下的精准贡献归因，为数据估值、联邦学习和AI对齐等领域提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: We propose a framework for reward allocation in fully delegated AI cooperatives where humans are represented by agents that contribute data and participate in model updates under heterogeneous value constraints. The key idea is to credit only those updates that remain admissible after screening them against each principal's value profile. We formulate value-conditioned gradient filtering, online marginal contribution signals, and cumulative revenue settlement within a traversal learning (TL) substrate. TL is especially attractive here because it performs decentralized backpropagation without the quality loss associated with aggregation-centric distributed learning and, we argue, offers a finer attribution substrate than FedAvg-style federated learning by preserving explicit traversal and gradient paths. The framework is positioned against data valuation, federated contribution estimation, personalized federated learning, and pluralistic alignment.

</details>


### [48] [Prism Transformer: Progressive Head Schedules for Hierarchical Attention Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27449)
*Shubham Aggarwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Prism Transformer，通过逐层增加注意力头数量的非均匀分配策略，在保持参数量和计算量不变的情况下，持续提升模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多头注意力机制在所有层均采用均匀的头维度分配(dh = dmodel/h)，这种静态配置限制了浅层头捕获复杂、高维上下文模式的能力，成为结构性的表示瓶颈。

Method: 引入Prism Transformer架构范式，采用渐进式头调度策略：浅层使用较少但维度宽的头捕获复杂局部组合模式，深层部署多个窄头将这些模式分解为专门的语义特征，构建局部到全局的表示层次。该结构在参数量、计算量和推理开销上均保持中性。

Result: 在三个模型规模(124M、354M、757M)上，Prism Transformer均持续超越均匀分配基线，验证损失持续降低，在PIQA、HellaSwag、ARC-Easy和WinoGrande等下游零样本基准测试中均获得稳定提升。

Conclusion: 非均匀子空间分配能够释放标准Transformer预算内的潜在容量，实现模型容量的更有效利用，证明了打破传统均匀头配置约束的可行性和有效性。

Abstract: Multi-head attention conventionally partitions the hidden dimension equally across all heads at every layer, enforcing an identical representational subspace dimension (dh = dmodel/h) throughout the models depth. In this work, we identify this uniform allocation as a fundamental structural bottleneck: due to their restricted dimensional space, early-layer heads are unable to faithfully capture complex, high-dimensional contextual patterns. To resolve this, we introduce the Prism Transformer, a novel architectural paradigm that replaces the static, uniform head configuration with a progressive head schedule. By monotonically increasing the head count across layers, the Prism Transformer naturally establishes a local-to-global representational hierarchy: early layers leverage fewer, exceptionally wide heads to capture complex, local compositional patterns, while deep layers deploy many, narrow heads to decompose these patterns into specialized linguistic features. Crucially, this structural shift is parameter-neutral, compute-neutral, and introduces zero training or inference overhead, preserving identical weight matrices and FLOP budgets as the standard Transformer. Across three model scales (124M, 354M, and 757M), the Prism Transformer consistently outperforms uniform baselines, achieving consistent reductions in validation loss alongside consistent gains on downstream zero-shot benchmarks (including PIQA, HellaSwag, ARC-Easy, and WinoGrande). Our findings demonstrate that non-uniform subspace allocation unlocks latent capacity within the standard Transformer budget, enabling more effective use of model capacity.

</details>


### [49] [Operator Learning for Cubic Nonlinear Schrödinger Equation on Periodic Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27459)
*Emmanuel E. Oguadimma,Victory C. Obieke,Xueying Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种几何条件化的傅里叶神经算子(FNO)用于求解二维环面上的立方非线性薛定谔方程，通过引入纵横比参数ω²作为输入，模型能够成功捕捉不同几何构型下解的动力学行为差异，数值实验表明该方法在保持预测精度的同时有效再现了有理和无理环面上不同的Sobolev范数行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 立方非线性薛定谔方程在二维平坦环面上的解的动力学行为依赖于环面的纵横比，该参数决定了傅里叶共振结构，导致有理和无理几何构型展现出不同的高频能量级联行为。传统数值方法难以捕捉这种几何依赖的谱转移现象，因此需要开发能够感知几何结构的神经算子来学习此类非线性色散偏微分方程中的谱转移现象。

Method: 作者提出了几何条件化的傅里叶神经算子(FNO)，其输入包含解的实部和虚部以及纵横比参数ω²，模型被训练用于近似单步解算子。采用傅里叶伪谱方法从随机相位初始数据生成训练数据，评估在未见过的轨迹上进行。通过消融实验研究了保留傅里叶模式数量、激活函数、傅里叶层深度以及显式几何条件化的作用。

Result: 数值实验表明，学习到的算子在两种环面几何构型上都能捕捉主要的解动力学行为，并成功复现了两种几何的Sobolev范数行为差异：有理环面上表现出更强的H²增长，而无理环面上行为更为受限，这与已有文献的发现一致。消融实验结果显示，包含ω²参数显著提升了长时间预测精度，特别是对于有理几何构型。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持在非线性色散偏微分方程中学习谱转移现象时使用几何感知神经算子，显式引入几何参数ω²能够改善长时间预测准确性，证明了几何条件化方法的有效性。该方法为处理具有几何依赖动力学行为的偏微分方程提供了新的思路。

Abstract: We consider the cubic nonlinear Schrödinger (NLS) equation on two-dimensional flat tori with varying aspect ratios. In this formulation, the choice of aspect ratio governs the Fourier resonance structure, so rational and irrational geometries can exhibit different high-frequency cascade behaviors. We present a geometry-conditioned Fourier neural operator (FNO) for the cubic defocusing NLS equation, where the input consists of the real and imaginary parts of the solution together with the aspect-ratio parameter \(ω^2\). The model is trained to approximate the one-step solution operator and is evaluated on unseen trajectories generated from random-phase initial data using Fourier pseudospectral method. Our numerical experiments show that the learned operator captures the main solution dynamics on both tori and reproduces the distinct Sobolev norm behavior of the two geometries, with stronger \(H^2\)-growth on the rational torus and more constrained behavior on the irrational torus, consistent with the findings of \cite{hrabski2021energy}. We perform ablation studies to examine the roles of retained Fourier modes, activation functions, Fourier-layer depth, and explicit geometry conditioning. The results indicate that including $ω^2$ improves long-time predictive accuracy, especially for the rational geometry, and supports the use of geometry-aware neural operators for learning spectral-transfer phenomena in nonlinear dispersive partial differential equations.

</details>


### [50] [Estimation--Prediction Tradeoff in Causal Probabilistic Temporal Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28225)
*Aniq Ur Rahman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了时序链接预测中存在“估计-预测”权衡：提高参数可恢复性的机制同时也增加了预测的不确定性，导致仅凭预测精度无法判断模型是否真正学习了潜在因果机制。作者提出了概率因果框架并推导了Cramér-Rao界来验证该权衡，指出需区分可约减的模型误差与过程固有不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时序链接预测评估通常基于未观测边的预测性能，但在概率时序图中，这一标准可能混淆模型误差与不可约减的不确定性。作者旨在研究并解决这一评估局限性，区分模型是否真正学习了潜在的因果机制。

Method: 首先，在二元逻辑模型中刻画了估计-预测权衡的理论性质。其次，提出了一种概率因果框架，用于生成具有瞬态边和已知真实因果结构的时序图。最后，针对提出的二元逻辑参数化方法，推导了Cramér-Rao界，以验证参数估计误差与不可约预测损失之间的权衡。

Result: 实验结果证实了参数估计误差与不可约预测损失之间存在理论预测的权衡关系。结果表明，仅依赖预测精度可能会误导对模型性能的判断，高预测误差并不一定意味着模型未能学习底层机制，反之亦然。

Conclusion: 仅凭预测精度不足以反映模型是否学习了底层因果机制。未来的基准测试需要能够区分可约减的模型误差与内在过程不确定性，从而更准确地评估时序图模型的真实学习能力。

Abstract: Temporal link prediction is usually evaluated by predictive performance on unseen edges, but in probabilistic temporal graphs this criterion can conflate model error with irreducible uncertainty. We study this issue by characterising an inherent estimation--prediction tradeoff in binary logistic models where regimes that maximise Fisher information and improve parameter recoverability are also those with the highest entropy, making individual predictions intrinsically harder even under perfect parameter recovery. We propose a probabilistic causal framework for generating temporal graphs with transient edges and known ground-truth causal structure, allowing temporal link prediction to be evaluated jointly with causal parameter recovery. For the proposed binary logistic parametrisation, we derive the Cramér--Rao bound and validate the tradeoff between parameter estimation error and irreducible predictive loss. Our results show that predictive accuracy alone may not reflect whether a model has learned the underlying causal mechanism, motivating benchmarks that distinguish reducible model error from intrinsic process uncertainty.

</details>


### [51] [The Curse of Multiple Mediators: Hidden Interaction Effects in Activation Patching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27510)
*Sankaran Vaidyanathan,David Arbour,Aaron Mueller,Scott Niekum,David Jensen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 激活修补中的自然间接效应（NIE）包含交互效应（INT），这一不可避免的现象揭示了组件因果效应的条件依赖性，应作为可解释性研究的诊断工具而非干扰项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激活修补是机制可解释性的主要工具，通过估计自然间接效应（NIE）来归因模型行为的因果责任。然而，NIE是否真正捕捉了特定组件的因果效应尚不明确，这促使研究者重新审视其理论基础。

Method: 从因果中介分析重新推导激活修补的估计量，理论分析NIE的组成；在GPT-2 IOI电路中实证演示失败模式；数学证明INT的性质，包括其与激活距离的关系、局部仿射条件下的行为及组合分解特性。

Result: 发现NIE包含交互效应（INT），其衡量组件因果效应对其他组件状态的依赖程度。INT导致条件重要组件被忽视或人为夸大，并解释了忠实度评分的不稳定性。INT随干净激活与修补激活间距离缩放，在局部仿射时可忽略，可组合分解为成对和更高阶群组交互。

Conclusion: INT不可通过调整估计量或分析单位消除，但它是可解释性研究的宝贵诊断工具。其大小和符号信号指示因果结论的提示依赖性及贪心组件排序的局限性，提示需要组合搜索来发现完整机制。

Abstract: Activation patching is the primary tool in mechanistic interpretability. It attributes causal responsibility for a model behavior to each of its individual components by estimating its natural indirect effect (NIE). Re-deriving the activation patching estimand from causal mediation analysis, we find that the NIE does not solely capture the causal effect through the specific component. It also contains interaction effects (INT) that measure how much the component's causal effect itself depends on the state of other components in the model. A natural response may be to try to eliminate INT by adjusting the estimator or unit of analysis, but each of these potential remedies has predictable failure modes. We demonstrate these failure modes in the GPT-2 IOI circuit; components whose causal importance is conditional on the state of other components are either invisible or artificially inflated, and INT variance explains the previously documented instability of faithfulness scores. We prove that INT scales with the distance between clean and patched component activations, is negligible when the model is locally affine, and decomposes combinatorially into pairwise and higher-order group interactions. Despite its inevitability, INT is not a nuisance to be eliminated, but rather a diagnostic for interpretability studies. Its individual and group-level magnitude and sign signal when causal conclusions are prompt-dependent, and when greedy NIE-based component ranking will miss mechanisms only discoverable through combinatorial search.

</details>


### [52] [Boundary condition fidelity for bottom-hole pressure and CO2 plume prediction in geological carbon storage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27515)
*Romal Ramadhan,Seyyed A. Hosseini,Larry W. Lake*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究评估了地质碳储存模拟中截断域边界处理对井底压力和CO2羽流预测精度的影响，发现角点孔隙体积校正是关键因素，结合渗透率修正的渐进式修正方法可实现最优预测精度（压力NRMSE<3.7%，羽流IoU>0.97）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地质碳储存安全性的关键在于准确预测井底压力(BHP)和CO2羽流迁移，但实际模拟常采用截断域，其中人工边界会扭曲压力扩散和CO2饱和度分布，需要系统评估边界条件保真度对预测精度的影响。

Method: 对比分析了十种简化域边界处理方法与全域参考模拟结果，测试了均匀孔隙体积乘数、渗透率修正因子、角点孔隙体积校正、分层校正和渐进式修正等方法，在均质和非均质储层中使用BHP的RMSE、NRMSE、峰值压力偏差和羽流交并比(IoU)作为性能评价指标。

Result: 角点孔隙体积守恒是截断域建模的最重要要求。忽略角点存储的均匀处理方法产生较大压力误差（均质模型BHP RMSE为362-382 psi，非均质模型为250-304 psi），羽流IoU约为0.80-0.84。角点校正方案显著降低压力误差，IoU提升至0.94以上。渗透率修正在均质储层中改善压力保真度，但在非均质储层中可能过度限制变渗透率边界面的流动。渐进式修正结合渗透率修正提供最一致的性能，BHP NRMSE低于3.7%，羽流IoU高于0.97。

Conclusion: 截断域边界处理的最佳实践是确保角点孔隙体积守恒，并采用渐进式修正结合渗透率校正，该方法在均质和非均质储层中均能实现高精度的压力和羽流预测，为地质碳储存的安全评估提供可靠的技术支撑。

Abstract: Accurate prediction of bottom-hole pressure (BHP) and CO2 plume migration is essential for safe geological carbon storage, yet practical simulations often rely on truncated domains where artificial boundaries distort pressure diffusion and CO2 saturation footprints. In this study, we evaluate how boundary-condition fidelity affects BHP and CO2 plume prediction by comparing ten reduced-domain boundary treatments against full-domain reference simulations in homogeneous and heterogeneous reservoirs. We test uniform pore-volume multipliers, transmissibility modifiers, corner-adjusted pore-volume corrections, layered corrections, and gradual modifiers using BHP RMSE, NRMSE, peak pressure deviation, and plume Intersection over Union (IoU) as performance metrics. Our results show that conserving corner pore volume is the most important requirement for truncated-domain modeling. We find that uniform treatments which neglect corner storage generate large pressure errors, with BHP RMSE of 362 to 382 psi in the homogeneous model and 250 to 304 psi in the heterogeneous model, and yield plume IoU values near 0.80 to 0.84, indicating roughly 16 to 20% of the combined plume area is misrepresented. Corner-adjusted scenarios substantially reduce pressure errors and raise plume IoU above 0.94, but we observe that transmissibility correction is not universally beneficial. In homogeneous reservoirs, uniform transmissibility adjustment improves pressure fidelity; in heterogeneous reservoirs, it can over-restrict flow across variable-permeability boundary faces, increasing BHP error and contracting the predicted plume. We find the gradual modifier with transmissibility correction provides the most consistent performance, achieving BHP NRMSE below 3.7% and plume IoU above 0.97 in both reservoir types.

</details>


### [53] [Democratic ICAI: Debating Our Way to Steering Principles from Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28294)
*Kevin Kingslin,Anish Natekar,Ashutosh Ranjan,Vivek Srivastava,Savita Bhat,Shirish Karande*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Democratic ICAI方法，通过结构化角色辩论收集多样化的决策理由，生成更清晰全面的引导原则，在偏好预测任务上优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于偏好的对齐方法难以捕捉人类判断背后的推理过程。成对标签仅揭示最终选择而非塑造偏好的考量因素；而逆宪法AI（ICAI）的单轮解释在复杂决策中会遗漏大量细微差别。

Method: 提出Democratic ICAI新方法：1）通过结构化角色辩论收集多个相互竞争的决策理由；2）从这些丰富的信号中推导出更清晰、更全面的引导原则；3）利用这些原则通过LLM和决策树模型引导决策建模。

Result: 在MuCE-Pref和LiTBench创意偏好基准测试的多个创意任务类别上，Democratic ICAI表现出更忠实的偏好结构。相比审议式提示和基于原则的基线方法，平均偏好预测能力提升，且生成的宪法原则被LLM标注者偏好。

Conclusion: Democratic ICAI通过结构化辩论机制有效解决了现有偏好对齐方法在捕捉决策推理方面的局限性，为复杂决策提供了更具表现力和更全面的偏好建模方案。

Abstract: Preference-based alignment often struggles to capture the reasoning that underlies human judgments. Many evaluations rely on multiple interacting criteria, yet pairwise labels reveal only the final choice rather than the considerations that shape preferences. Inverse Constitutional AI (ICAI) improves interpretability in decision making by summarizing preferences into natural-language principles, but its single-pass explanations miss much of the nuance involved in complex decisions. We introduce Democratic ICAI, a novel approach that gathers multiple competing rationales through structured persona debate, offering a broader and more expressive account of the factors influencing each comparison. From these richer signals, we derive clearer and more comprehensive steering principles and use them to guide decision modeling through both LLM-based and decision-tree judges. Experiments on creative preference benchmarks, MuCE-Pref and LiTBench, across multiple creative task categories show that Democratic ICAI yields a more faithful preference structure. It improves average preference prediction across tasks relative to deliberative prompting and principle-based baselines, while producing constitutions that LLM annotators prefer.

</details>


### [54] [Productionized Fairness Measurement Under Privacy Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27558)
*Osonde A. Osoba,Yuzi He,Saikrishna Badrinarayanan,Varun Mithal,Sakshi Jain,Natesh S. Pillai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了PPRE方法，通过隐私保护技术实现LinkedIn美国成员的种族/族裔公平性测量，解决了公平性评估中人口统计信号难以获取和使用的难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的公平性测量依赖于人口统计信号（如种族和族裔），这些信号往往受到法律限制或具有文化敏感性，难以收集和使用，限制了公平性评估的实施。

Method: 提出隐私保护概率种族/族裔估计（PPRE）方法，将隐私技术（安全两方计算、差分隐私、加法同态加密）应用于两种种族/族裔人口信号源（贝叶斯改进姓氏地理编码估计器和稀疏金标准调查集），构建隐私保护的公平性测量解决方案。

Result: 详细阐述了PPRE的隐私保证，并在LinkedIn平台上验证了其在候选人端和查看者端公平性测量中的有效性应用。

Conclusion: 为寻求实施类似隐私保护测量基础设施的机构提供了可迁移的框架和实施指南。

Abstract: Fairness measurements in the form of disaggregated evaluations often rely on demographic signals that are legally constrained or culturally sensitive. Race and ethnicity signals are among the more difficult signals to curate and use for this task. This paper presents Privacy-Preserving Probabilistic Race/Ethnicity Estimation (PPRE) as a method for enabling fairness measurements with respect to race/ethnicity for U.S.\ LinkedIn members in a privacy-preserving manner. PPRE applies privacy technologies (specifically: secure two-party computation, differential privacy, and additive homomorphic encryption) on top of two race/ethnicity demographic signal sources (the Bayesian Improved Surname Geocoding estimator and a sparse golden survey set of self-reported demographics) to power a fairness measurement solution with respect to US-based race/ethnicity demographics. We detail its privacy guarantees and demonstrate its application on candidate- and viewer-side fairness measurements. We close with a transferable framework for institutions seeking to implement similar privacy-preserving measurement infrastructure.

</details>


### [55] [Quantum Generative Diffusion Model for Real-World Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27561)
*Jack Waller,Filippo Caruso,Dimitrios Makris,Rajagopal Nilavalan,Xing Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个用于时间序列合成的量子生成扩散模型QDiffusion-TS，在IQM量子处理器上验证显示：相比经典模型参数减少近三个数量级，Wasserstein距离降低约44%，下游预测任务RMSE提升高达71%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型虽然取得了显著成功，但模型规模增大带来的计算成本和效率问题日益突出。量子机器学习提供了一种有前景的替代方案，能够用紧凑且高表达能力的模型表示复杂数据分布。

Method: 提出QDiffusion-TS框架，通过用量子神经网络替换去噪transformer中的前馈组件来扩展经典扩散架构，构建混合量子transformer，从而大幅减少可训练参数数量。

Result: 在Apple和Amazon金融时间序列数据上验证：(1)每个替换组件的可训练参数减少近三个数量级；(2)相比经典模型，Wasserstein距离降低约44%；(3)在下游预测任务中，使用生成数据进行增强相比仅用真实数据训练的基线，RMSE提升高达71%。

Conclusion: 量子增强架构能够以显著更少的参数一致地匹敌甚至超越经典性能，为更高效和可扩展的数据驱动生成建模建立了实用框架。

Abstract: Generative models have achieved remarkable success in data synthesis, though recent advances driven by increasing model scale have introduced challenges in computational cost and efficiency. Quantum machine learning offers a promising alternative, representing complex data distributions using compact, highly expressive models. Here, we propose QDiffusion-TS, the first quantum generative diffusion model for time series synthesis, and validate it on the IQM quantum processor. The framework extends a classical diffusion architecture by replacing feed-forward components within the denoising transformer with quantum neural networks, yielding a hybrid quantum transformer that reduces the number of trainable parameters in each replaced component by nearly three orders of magnitude. Evaluated on financial time series from Apple and Amazon, the model generates synthetic data that more accurately reproduces the real distributions, reducing Wasserstein distance by approximately 44% relative to its classical counterpart across both datasets. In a downstream forecasting task, augmentation with the generated data improves predictive performance by up to 71% in RMSE over a baseline trained solely on real data. These results show that quantum enhanced architectures can consistently match and frequently surpass classical performance with substantially fewer parameters, establishing a practical framework towards more efficient and scalable data-driven generative modelling.

</details>


### [56] [hia-gat: A Heterogeneous Interaction-Aware Graph Attention Network For Frame-Level Traffic Conflict Risk Prediction On Freeways](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27577)
*Mahshid Malazizi,Seyedmehdi Khaleghian,Mina Sartipi,Toru Hirano,Yunfei Xu,Hoang H. Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HIA-GAT双流异构图注意力网络用于高速公路风险评估，在NGSIM数据集上达到最佳平均风险排序性能（I-80: AUC 0.835, US-101: AUC 0.867），尤其在换道场景表现突出，并提供可解释的冲突类型归因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高速公路风险评估方法未能充分利用车辆间的交互关系结构，特别是在换道冲突等需要关系建模的场景中表现不佳。需要一种能够同时建模纵向和横向交互、且具有可解释性的风险评估方法。

Method: 将帧级高速公路风险建模为多智能体场景图二分类问题，构建关系感知图（车辆为节点，同车道和相邻车道交互为边）；提出HIA-GAT双流异构图注意力网络，通过专用注意力路径处理纵向和横向交互，利用冲突类型感知门控机制进行融合，并采用SSM冲突归因提供事件级门控监督。

Result: 在NGSIM I-80和US-101数据集的九种TTC和PET阈值配置实验中，HIA-GAT取得最佳平均风险排序性能（I-80: AUC 0.835, US-101: AUC 0.867），在PET-only换道设置中提升最大；图结构对横向冲突风险建模至关重要，而纵向风险可通过非关系聚合捕获。

Conclusion: 图结构对高速公路风险评估不可或缺，特别是对横向冲突风险建模；HIA-GAT不仅提升预测准确性，其学习到的门控机制还提供了可解释的每车主导冲突类型归因，支持可操作的实时高速公路安全监控。

Abstract: This paper formulates frame-level freeway risk assessment as a multi-agent scene graph-level binary classification problem, where each video or trajectory frame is labeled risky if any TTC- or PET-based conflict violates a specified severity threshold. We construct a relation-aware graph per frame with vehicles as nodes and two interaction types as edges: same-lane (longitudinal) and adjacent-lane (lateral), augmented with physics-informed edge features aligned to rear-end and lane-change conflict mechanisms. Building on a structured benchmarking suite of non-graph models and graph baselines, we propose HIA-GAT, a dual-stream heterogeneous graph attention network that processes longitudinal and lateral interactions through dedicated attention pathways and fuses them via a conflict-type-aware gating mechanism with event-level gate supervision derived from SSM conflict attribution. Experiments on the NGSIM I-80 and US-101 freeway datasets across nine TTC and PET threshold configurations show that HIA-GAT achieves the best average risk-ranking performance (AUC 0.835 on I-80 and 0.867 on US-101), with the largest gains on PET-only (lane-change) settings where relational structure is essential. Beyond accuracy, the learned gate provides interpretable per-vehicle attribution of dominant conflict type, supporting actionable, real-time freeway safety monitoring. We show that graph structure is critical for modeling lateral conflict risk, while longitudinal risk can often be captured by non-relational aggregation.

</details>


### [57] [PEBS: Per-rater Empirical-Bayes Shrinkage for RLHF Reward-Model Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27578)
*Arnav Raj*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PEBS是一种针对RLHF奖励模型的个体化仿射校准方法，通过经验贝叶斯收缩估计器实现，在不重新训练奖励模型的情况下显著降低了预测误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的RLHF奖励模型将成千上万名标注者的偏好进行汇总，拟合一个全局仿射校准器，这种方式忽略了不同评分者之间存在系统性差异（评分尺度偏移和斜率差异），将所有评分者压缩为一个平均评分者拟合，无法准确匹配任何个体标注者的特征。

Method: PEBS（Per-rater Empirical-Bayes Shrinkage）方法：1）为每个评分者拟合独立的仿射校准器，使用每个标注者评分的留出切片；2）应用Morris-James-Stein经验贝叶斯收缩，向总体均值收缩；3）以闭式形式实现，无需重新训练奖励模型；4）仅估计推理时使用的评分者级别映射，保持奖励基础模型不变。

Result: 在PRISM数据集上，PEBS相比汇总群体斜率基线降低了8.58%的组内留出RMSE；在PluriHarms危害评分数据集上（使用Qwen-2.5基础模型、同族设置），相比相同基线实现了9.66%的RMSE降低。

Conclusion: PEBS是一种有效的闭式后验估计器，能够在不改变奖励基础模型的前提下，通过个体化仿射校准显著提升RLHF奖励建模的准确性，解决了传统方法忽略评分者个体差异的问题。

Abstract: Reward models for Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) pool preferences across thousands of annotators and fit one global affine calibrator, collapsing raters with systematically different rating-scale offsets and slopes into a single average-rater fit that does not match any individual annotator. PEBS is a per-rater empirical-Bayes shrinkage estimator: it fits per-rater affine calibrators on a held-out slice of each annotator's ratings and applies Morris-James-Stein empirical-Bayes shrinkage toward the population mean, in closed form and without retraining the reward model. On PRISM, PEBS reduces within-user held-out RMSE by 8.58% over the pooled population-slope baseline. The procedure replicates on PluriHarms harm ratings (Qwen-2.5 base, in-family) with a +9.66% RMSE reduction over the same population-slope baseline. PEBS is a closed-form post-hoc estimator for annotator-specific affine calibration in RLHF reward modeling; it leaves the reward base model unchanged and estimates only the rater-level map used at inference time for new ratings.

</details>


### [58] [Retroactive Advantage Correction: Closed-Form V-Trace Bias Correction for Delay-Aware RLHF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27580)
*Arnav Raj*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对RLHF中非同步奖励信号问题，提出RAC算法，通过延迟核重注入修正PPO的优势估计，在表格MDP中将策略偏差降低高达47.9倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境中的RLHF常面临非同步奖励信号问题：代码执行验证器、慢速评判集成和排队人工审查可能在策略rollout数个梯度步后才返回奖励，破坏了标准PPO的同步奖励假设。

Method: 提出Retroactive Advantage Correction (RAC)：将待处理的慢速完成项排队，通过非负核进行老化处理，作为截断残差重新注入到下一个优化器步的优势估计中。在无偏截断重要性比率下证明累积RAC修正的无偏性，与V-trace方法建立联系。

Result: 在表格MDP概念验证实验中，RAC在双慢速通道配置下将闭式策略偏差降低高达47.9倍，以更低的实际耗时超越等待慢速奖励的基线方法。算法仅需两行代码即可与PPO和GRPO集成。

Conclusion: RAC有效解决了RLHF生产环境中的异步奖励问题，提供了理论保证和实际性能提升，实现了算法简洁性和有效性的统一，为实际部署提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF) in production does not always have a synchronous reward signal. Code-execution verifiers, slow judge ensembles, and queued human review can return several gradient steps after the rollout that produced them, breaking the synchronous-reward assumption underlying standard PPO. We address this gap with Retroactive Advantage Correction (RAC): each pending slow completion is queued, aged through a non-negative kernel, and reinjected as a clipped residual into the next optimiser step's advantage. We prove that under an unbiased clipped importance ratio, the cumulative RAC correction is exactly unbiased when the effective delay kernel reinjects all of its mass, and carries a bias linear in the unreinjected fraction otherwise; at the no-delay identity kernel it reduces to V-trace. On a tabular Markov decision process (MDP) proof-of-concept, RAC reduces the closed-form policy bias by up to 47.9x at the two-slow-channel configuration, beating wait-for-slow at lower wall-clock cost. RAC integrates with PPO and GRPO through a two-line reward-manager patch.

</details>


### [59] [Global Explanations for Multivariate Time Series Forecasting Models via $K$-Order Markov Approximations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27599)
*Amadeo Tunyi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了KARMA方法，通过构建马尔可夫替代模型来解释时间序列预测模型，解决了现有XAI方法忽视时间依赖性的问题，并在合成数据和真实数据上验证了其优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的可解释AI方法大多不是为时间序列预测模型设计的，且隐含假设时间戳特征相互独立，忽略了时间依赖性这一基本属性，可能导致解释违反数据的序列和因果结构。

Method: 提出了KARMA方法，通过构建马尔可夫替代模型来捕捉预测器学习到的时间依赖性。方法包含三个主要方面：(1)识别对模型预测充分的最小历史长度K；(2)从离散化历史空间估计最佳拟合的K阶马尔可夫转移核；(3)从马尔可夫转移核推导出五级全局解释层次结构。

Result: 在复杂合成数据上的受控实验证明KARMA能够：(i)恢复模型学习到的数据因果结构；(ii)比TimeSHAP等现有归因方法更好地识别时间依赖性。使用北京PM2.5真实天气数据进行了方法演示。

Conclusion: KARMA成功解决了时间序列预测模型解释中的时间依赖性问题，提供了一种能够捕捉序列和因果结构的解释方法，在识别时间依赖性方面优于现有的归因方法。

Abstract: While many explainable AI (XAI) methods have been proposed, most are not designed for time-series forecasting models and often rely on the implicit assumption that timestamp features are independent. This assumption ignores the fundamental property of temporal dependence and can lead to explanations that violate the sequential and causal structure of the data. We introduce \textsc{KARMA}, a method for explaining time-series predictors by constructing a Markov surrogate model that captures the temporal dependencies learned by the predictor. Our approach revolves around three main aspects: identifying the minimal history length $K$ that is predictively sufficient for the model, estimating the best-fitting $K$-order Markov transition kernel from the discretized history space, and a five-level global explanation hierarchy that can be derived from the Markov transition kernel, which we illustrate using real-world weather data (Beijing PM 2.5). We also certify using complex synthetic data with known true causal edges that KARMA (i) recovers the data causal structure as learned by the model via a controlled experiment and (ii) identifies temporal dependencies better than established attribution methods such as TimeSHAP.

</details>


### [60] [Training Observable Control Policies to Expose Agent State Through Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27609)
*Andres Enriquez Fernandez,John J. Bird*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在通信受限情况下，利用强化学习训练智能体策略，使其动作具有可观测性，从而实现对智能体状态的估计，且对任务性能影响最小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在自主智能体系统中，物理或操作约束常导致通信受限，使得监控或多智能体协调变得困难。即使在缺乏强通信的情况下，智能体的动作仍可作为信息源，因为智能体与环境交互时的动作可被观测，这为状态估计提供了新的途径。

Method: 采用强化学习方法开发策略，通过在训练奖励中引入策略可观测性激励，使智能体的动作更易于被观测和用于状态估计。通过仿真训练后的智能体来分析策略的可观测性。

Result: 在飞机跟踪问题中，成功找到了一种具有增强可观测性的策略，该策略对名义任务性能的影响极小，证明了通过动作观测进行状态估计的可行性。

Conclusion: 通过强化学习训练具有可观测性激励的策略，可以在通信受限环境下有效实现智能体状态估计，且不会显著牺牲任务性能，为多智能体协调和监控提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Physical or operational constraints often impose communications limitations on autonomous agents. Such limitations complicate monitoring or multiagent coordination. Even when strong communications are absent, some information may still be available. The remainder of the relevant agent state may be reconstructed via estimation. The actions taken by an agent are a potential source of information -- as the agent interacts with the environment, these actions may be observed even in the absence of explicit communication. We investigate using actions to estimate the state of an agent, using reinforcement learning to develop policies which make the estimation problem more tractable. Policy observability is encouraged through the training reward and is analyzed using simulation of the trained agent. In an aircraft tracking problem a policy with enhanced observability is found that has minimal impact on nominal task performance.

</details>


### [61] [COOPA: A Modular LLM Agent Architecture for Operations Research Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27611)
*Chuanhao Li,Xiaoan Xu,Dirk Bergemann,Ethan X. Fang,Yehua Wei,Zhuoran Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了COOPA，一种模块化的LLM智能体架构，用于可解释和可扩展的运筹学决策支持，在六个backbone上取得了最佳准确率，相比最强基线提升高达6.7个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运筹学(OR)为高风险决策提供了严格的框架，但有效的OR建模需要大量的领域知识、数学抽象和求解器专业知识。现有的基于LLM的系统虽然自动化了部分流程，但在复杂问题上准确率低、输出不透明且求解器支持有限。

Method: COOPA结合三个核心组件：(1)迭代置信度建模，生成多个候选公式，在建模维度上自我评估，并使用最大-最小置信度准则选择一个；(2)元素级溯源和置信度解释，将变量、参数、约束和目标链接到引用的源文本，为人类验证提供审计追踪；(3)多求解器路由到针对不同OR问题类的专门优化器智能体。

Result: 在三个OR基准、八个LLM骨干和四个基线的相同条件下，COOPA在八个骨干中的六个上取得了最佳宏平均准确率，相比最强基线提升高达6.7个百分点。系统内消融实验分离了迭代置信度建模的贡献。

Conclusion: COOPA通过模块化架构成功解决了OR建模中的准确性、可解释性和求解器多样性问题，源可追溯性和多求解器调度的价值通过额外分析和案例研究得到了验证。

Abstract: Operations Research (OR) provides a rigorous framework for high-stakes decision-making, but effective OR modeling requires substantial domain knowledge, mathematical abstraction, and solver expertise. Recent LLM-based systems automate parts of this pipeline, yet remain limited by low accuracy on complex problems, opaque outputs, and narrow solver support. We propose COOPA (COoperative OPerations Agent), a modular LLM-agent architecture for interpretable and scalable OR decision support. It combines three components: iterative confidence-based modeling, which generates multiple candidate formulations, self-evaluates them across modeling dimensions, and selects one using a max-min confidence criterion; element-level provenance and confidence explanations, which link variables, parameters, constraints, and objectives to quoted source text and provide an audit trail for human verification; and multi-solver routing to specialized optimizer agents for different OR problem classes. Across three OR benchmarks, eight LLM backbones, and four baselines under identical conditions, COOPA achieves the best macro-average accuracy on six of eight backbones and improves over the strongest baseline by up to 6.7 percentage points. A within-system ablation isolates the contribution of iterative confidence-based modeling, while additional analyses and case studies illustrate the value of source traceability and multi-solver dispatch.

</details>


### [62] [HybridCodec: Modeling Discrete and Continuous Representations for Efficient Speech Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27627)
*Artem Ploujnikov,Francesco Verdini,Samir Sadok,Mirco Ravanelli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合时间压缩离散token和降维连续残差的混合方法，解决了音频离散化导致的信息丢失问题，在保持说话人特征的同时减少了自回归步骤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的离散音频表示在构建多模态文本-音频系统和集成到大语言模型时，由于离散化过程中的信息丢失，导致各种下游任务性能下降。

Method: 提出混合离散-连续焦点调制编解码器和混合Transformer架构。该框架在离散域进行自回归推理，同时结合非自回归预测和连续残差上采样。

Result: 实验结果表明，相比纯离散方法，该方法显著改善了说话人特征的保留，同时减少了所需的自回归步骤数量。

Conclusion: 该混合离散-连续表示方法有效缓解了音频离散化带来的信息损失问题，在性能和效率之间取得了更好的平衡，为音频大语言模型提供了更优的解决方案。

Abstract: Discrete audio representations have become increasingly popular for building multimodal text-audio systems and integrating audio capabilities into Large Language Models (LLMs). However, numerous studies report performance degradation on various downstream tasks due to information loss during discretization. To address this, we propose a novel approach combining temporally compressed discrete tokens with dimensionality-reduced continuous residuals. Our framework consists of a hybridized discrete-continuous focal modulation codec and a hybrid Transformer. This architecture performs autoregressive inference in the discrete domain, coupled with non-autoregressive prediction and continuous residual upsampling. Experimental results show that our approach significantly improves the retention of speaker characteristics compared to discrete-only methods, while simultaneously reducing the number of required autoregressive steps.

</details>


### [63] [Continual Learning for Sequential Personalization of Small Language Models: A Stability Monitoring Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27634)
*Thomas S. Paula,Lucas S. Kupssinskü,Rodrigo C. Barros*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种基于检查点的评估协议，通过监测参考集分布偏移来诊断小型语言模型（SLMs）在顺序LoRA个性化过程中的不稳定性，发现仅依赖任务级指标可能掩盖有害的适应性变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型语言模型（SLMs）在边缘设备上的部署日益增多，需要随时间适应用户或任务特定数据进行个性化，这使其处于持续学习环境中。然而，持续学习存在灾难性遗忘风险，即学习新信息会降低先前任务的性能。现有基准测试（如TRACE）表明持续微调会显著降低对齐大语言模型的通用能力。

Method: 提出了一种顺序LoRA个性化评估方法：在每个适应阶段后保存模型检查点，并在当前任务、先前任务和固定参考集上进行评估。这种检查点级协议能够监测任务性能、遗忘程度和参考集漂移随时间的变化，并开发了轻量级参考集分布诊断方法。

Result: 研究发现轻量级参考集分布诊断能够揭示SLMs在顺序LoRA个性化过程中的模型特定不稳定性模式，包括某些情况下任务级指标单独无法反映的有害适应性变化。

Conclusion: 该研究为监测SLMs在持续学习环境下的稳定性提供了新的诊断视角，突出了在这一领域开展新研究方向的重要性，有助于开发更可靠的边缘设备个性化模型。

Abstract: Small Language Models (SLMs) are increasingly being considered for deployment on edge devices such as laptops, enabling private, low-latency, and locally personalized applications. However, personalization requires models to adapt over time to evolving user- or task-specific data, placing them in a continual learning setting. This creates the risk of catastrophic forgetting, where learning new information degrades performance on previously learned tasks or broader model capabilities. Recent benchmarks such as TRACE have shown that continual fine-tuning can significantly degrade the general abilities of aligned large language models. In this work, we present a study for sequential LoRA personalization of SLMs. We save model checkpoints after each adaptation stage and evaluate them on current tasks, previously seen tasks, and a fixed reference set. This checkpoint-level protocol enables us to monitor task performance, forgetting, and reference set drift over time. We show that lightweight reference set distributional diagnostics can reveal model-specific instability patterns during sequential LoRA personalization of SLMs, including cases where task-level metrics alone hide harmful adaptation. We hope this can highlight new research avenues for monitoring stability of SLMs in a continual learning setting.

</details>


### [64] [TeRoR: Decoupled Temporal Rotation with Relational Circular Region for Temporal Knowledge Graph Embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27651)
*Peijia Xie,Yike Liu,Chao He,Huiling Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TeRoR方法，通过独立旋转变换和半径约束改进时序知识图谱嵌入，解决了TeRo在关系映射属性和时序信息表达上的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TeRo方法在建模时序知识图谱时存在两个主要缺陷：（1）无法有效处理多种关系映射属性（一对多、多对一、多对多）；（2）时序信息表达能力有限。

Method: 提出TeRoR方法，在复向量空间中对头尾实体分别进行独立旋转变换以增强时序建模能力；同时训练半径参数，将旋转平移后的头实体约束在以尾实体为中心的圆形区域内，从而有效捕获关系的多样性映射属性。

Result: 在四个不同的时序知识图谱数据集上，TeRoR达到了与最先进模型相当甚至更优的性能表现。

Conclusion: TeRoR通过改进时序信息建模和关系映射属性捕获能力，为时序知识图谱嵌入提供了更有效的解决方案，在多个基准数据集上验证了方法的有效性。

Abstract: In recent years, with the emergence of Temporal Knowledge Graphs (TKGs), research on learning entity and relation representations in TKGs has attracted increasing attention, giving rise to a large number of TKG embedding methods. TeRo is a simple and efficient temporal knowledge graph embedding approach. However, TeRo does not do well in modeling the mapping properties of various relations, such as one-to-many, many-to-one, and many-to-many. Meanwhile, it also has limitations in the expression of temporal information. To address these issues, we propose a novel TKG embedding method named TeRoR. This method divides the temporal evolution of entity embeddings, and conducts independent rotation transformations on head and tail entities in the complex vector space to strengthen temporal information modeling capacity. In terms of relational characteristics, we train a radius to constrain the rotated and translated head entities within a circular region centered on the tail entity, which effectively captures the diverse mapping properties of relations. Experimental results demonstrate that TeRoR achieves competitive performance against state-of-the-art models on four distinct TKG datasets.

</details>


### [65] [Are Time-Series Foundation Models Ready for E-Nose Data? An Empirical Assessment of Their Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27672)
*Taeyeong Choi,Mohammed Kamruzzaman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了时间序列基础模型（TSFMs）在电子鼻（E-Nose）气体传感数据上的表现，发现微调是必要的，且融合TSFM嵌入与专业模型可提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列基础模型（TSFMs）在自然语言处理和计算机视觉领域取得进展后，被提出具有跨领域强泛化能力的潜力。然而，其在气体传感数据（特别是电子鼻数据）上的应用价值尚未被探索，存在研究空白。

Method: 系统评估了代表性TSFMs（包括Chronos-2和MOMENT）在电子鼻数据上的表现。具体研究TSFMs生成的嵌入向量是否为气体识别和浓度预测提供有效表征，并探索微调和模型融合策略。

Result: 实验表明：1）TSFMs在E-Nose数据上必须进行微调才能达到令人满意的性能；2）将TSFM嵌入与专业预测模型学习的表征进行融合，可以进一步提升性能。这揭示了当前TSFMs在气体传感应用中的潜力与局限性。

Conclusion: 当前时间序列基础模型在气体传感领域具有应用潜力但存在局限，需要通过微调和模型融合策略来优化性能，为TSFMs在实际传感应用中的部署提供了重要见解。

Abstract: Inspired by advances in natural language processing and computer vision, "time-series foundation models" (TSFMs) have recently been introduced with the promise of strong generalization across diverse time-series tasks, including forecasting, classification, and anomaly detection, as well as across domains such as healthcare, climate science, and manufacturing. However, their utility for gas-sensing data remains largely unexplored. To address this gap, this paper systematically evaluates recent TSFMs on electronic nose (E-Nose) data. In particular, we investigate whether embeddings produced by representative TSFMs, including Chronos-2 and MOMENT, provide effective representations for gas identification and concentration prediction. Specifically, we show that fine-tuning is necessary to achieve satisfactory performance on E-Nose data, and fusing TSFM embeddings with representations learned by specialized predictive models can further improve the performance, suggesting both the potential and limitations of current TSFMs for gas-sensing applications.

</details>


### [66] [Textual Belief States for World Models: Identifiable Representation Learning Under Strict Mediation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27681)
*Xiang Gao,Kaiwen Dong,Yuguang Yao,Padmaja Jonnalagedda,Kamalika Das*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了文本潜在状态和fGRPO方法，通过强制严格潜在状态中介来解决LLM世界模型中的历史旁路问题，在文本环境中显著提升了表示质量和规划性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在部分可观测环境中，基于LLM的世界模型存在历史旁路问题：预测性能无法反映表示质量，导致潜在状态不可识别。现有的文本潜在状态是离散且不可微的，阻碍了变分训练，且表达性强的LLM解码器容易忽略瓶颈层。

Method: 提出文本潜在状态（离散、可解释、变长）和因子化GRPO（fGRPO）方法。fGRPO是一种树结构强化学习方法，在训练过程中强制执行严格潜在状态中介，即预测仅依赖于潜在状态和动作。研究同时形式化了严格中介的必要性，证明了它使表示质量可测试。

Result: 在TextWorld和ScienceWorld实验中，在保持单步预测准确率的同时，表示质量提升最高57%，rollout性能提升98%。性能提升随任务复杂度和时间跨度增加而增长。

Conclusion: 严格潜在状态中介原则可以成功应用于文本领域，解决了LLM世界模型中潜在状态不可识别的问题，使得表示质量可以通过经验测试，为构建更可靠的基于文本的世界模型提供了可行方案。

Abstract: World models in partially observed environments rely on latent representations that summarize interaction history, but in many modern LLM-based architectures predictive performance fails to reflect representation quality due to history bypass, rendering the latent state unidentifiable. Strict latent state mediation, requiring predictions to depend only on the latent state and action, is a classical principle that resolves this, but enforcing it in text-based settings is an open challenge: textual latent states are discrete and non-differentiable, precluding variational training, and expressive LLM decoders readily ignore the bottleneck. We show how to make strict mediation work in the text domain. We formalize why it is necessary, showing that strict mediation makes representation quality empirically testable while history-leaky architectures break this connection. We then introduce textual latent states, which are discrete, interpretable, and variable-length, and factorized GRPO (fGRPO), a tree-structured reinforcement learning method that enforces strict mediation during training. Experiments on TextWorld and ScienceWorld show preserved one-step prediction accuracy alongside up to 57\% gains in representation quality and 98\% improvements in rollout performance, increasing with task complexity and horizon.

</details>


### [67] [CBD: API-Only LLM Black-Box Unlearning through Controlled Behavioral Divergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27683)
*Zhiqiang Xie,Yijing Lin,Zhipeng Gao,Dong In Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了CBD框架，解决了仅API黑盒访问条件下的LLM机器遗忘问题，通过构建梯度统计判别基来区分目标和保留数据，在遗忘效果和模型效用之间实现了更好的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘设备通过API调用LLM时，边缘生成的数据可能包含敏感、受版权保护、有害或过时的信息，需要进行机器遗忘。现有方法面临两个关键挑战：1）仅API黑盒访问限制，无法获取目标模型参数和内部logits；2）当遗忘目标数据与保留数据具有高度相似的提示结构或语义模式时，如何保持模型效用。

Method: 提出受控行为发散（CBD）框架：1）使用两个辅助模型在保留输入和遗忘目标输入之间创建受控的行为发散；2）将发散转换为遗忘相关性分数，将与遗忘相关的提示从目标LLM路由开；3）通过估计经验Fisher矩阵并求解正则化广义特征值问题，构建基于梯度统计的判别基，引导遗忘信号指向目标特定信息而非共享的提示结构。

Result: 在TOFU forget10数据集上，CBD在遗忘集上接近重训练参考水平，同时将模型效用提升至74.90，比次优基线高约15%；在WMDP数据集上，将危险知识准确率降至25.68（接近随机猜测），同时保持MMLU准确率为52.67。与11个白盒和灰盒遗忘基线相比，CBD实现了更好的遗忘-效用权衡，且在不同设置下性能变化很小。

Conclusion: CBD框架成功解决了仅API黑盒访问条件下的机器遗忘问题，在高相似性数据场景下有效区分了目标特定信息和共享提示结构，实现了遗忘效果与模型效用的良好平衡，为边缘智能场景下的隐私保护和模型更新提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Edge devices increasingly invoke large language models (LLMs) through API services for context aware edge intelligence, while edge generated data may be collected to improve LLMs and may introduce sensitive, copyrighted, harmful, or outdated information into model behavior. Machine unlearning offers a practical way to remove the influence of undesired data without retraining LLMs. However, existing methods still face two gaps. The first is API only black box access, where target model parameters and internal logits are unavailable. The second is how to preserve retained utility when unlearning target data and retained data share highly similar prompt structures or semantic patterns. To address these challenges, we propose Controlled Behavioral Divergence (CBD), an API only black box unlearning framework. CBD uses two auxiliary models to create controlled behavioral divergence between retained inputs and unlearning target inputs, converts this divergence into an unlearning relevance score, and routes unlearning related prompts away from the target LLM. To improve discrimination accuracy under high similarity between target and retained data, CBD constructs a gradient statistics based discriminative basis by estimating empirical Fisher matrices and solving a regularized generalized eigenvalue problem, guiding the unlearning signal toward target specific information rather than shared prompt structures. Compared with eleven white box and gray box unlearning baselines, CBD achieves a better unlearning utility trade off and its performance varies little across settings. On ToFU forget10, CBD approaches the retrained reference on the forget set while raising model utility to 74.90, about 15% above the second best baseline. On WMDP, it lowers hazardous knowledge accuracy to 25.68, near random guessing, while preserving MMLU accuracy of 52.67. Code is at https://github.com/DGL-codes/CBD.

</details>


### [68] [Deployment-Side Adaptiveness in Multi-Horizon Volatility Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27688)
*Riku Green,Zahraa S. Abdallah,Telmo M Silva Filho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在多期限波动率预测中，改变推理时的展开规则可以从同一个训练好的MIMO模型诱导出一个预测家族；通过验证集选择的部署策略（单规则或小规模规则子集）能够在MSE目标下以较低推理成本优于默认部署，但策略排名对评价指标敏感（MSE vs QLIKE）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在金融预测中，预测性能不仅取决于训练的模型，还取决于模型如何被部署；同一训练好的多输出（MIMO）预测器通过改变推理时的展开规则可诱导出具有不同精度与成本特征的预测家族，而默认MIMO部署未必最优，且最佳固定规则在架构与预测期限间差异显著，因此需要研究如何系统地选择部署策略。

Method: 以多期限波动率预测为任务，系统研究推理时展开规则的变化对同一训练MIMO模型预测效果的影响；在20只股票波动率序列、三个预测期限、从线性模型到PatchTST的多种架构上进行实验；提出并评估基于验证集的部署策略（验证集选择的单例规则或小规模规则子集），并与默认MIMO部署和大规模集成方法对比；考察策略在不同评价指标（MSE vs QLIKE）下的表现差异。

Result: 非默认展开规则常优于标准MIMO部署；但最佳固定规则在不同架构和预测期限间差异很大，单一静态替换不可靠；在MSE目标下，验证集选择的单规则策略提供了低于默认MIMO的低成本改进，小规模规则子集能以显著更低的推理成本恢复大规模集成的绝大部分收益；策略排名对指标敏感：MSE选择的策略在QLIKE（金融标准波动率损失）上不一定一致有效。

Conclusion: 推理时部署策略是金融预测中可塑性的重要来源；训练好的波动率预测器应不仅按架构评估，还需按部署策略评估；验证集选择的部署策略可在控制推理成本的前提下提升预测效果，但策略选择需考虑目标评价指标。

Abstract: In financial forecasting, predictive performance depends not only on which model is trained, but also on how the trained model is deployed. We study this issue in multi-horizon volatility forecasting. Our starting point is that a trained multi-output (MIMO) forecaster does not define a single deployable predictor: by changing the inference-time rollout rule, the same trained model induces a family of forecasts with different accuracy and cost profiles. Across 20 stock-volatility series, three forecast horizons, and architectures ranging from linear models to PatchTST, we find that non-default rollout rules often improve over standard MIMO deployment. However, the best fixed rule varies substantially across architectures and horizons, making any single static replacement unreliable. We therefore evaluate validation-based deployment policies over the induced rule family. Under the primary MSE objective, validation-selected singletons provide a low-cost improvement over default MIMO, while small rule subsets recover much of the benefit of larger ensembles at substantially lower inference cost. We also find that policy rankings are metric-sensitive: MSE-selected policies do not transfer uniformly to QLIKE, a finance-standard volatility loss. These results show that inference-time deployment is a meaningful source of adaptiveness in financial forecasting, and that trained volatility forecasters should be evaluated not only by their architecture, but also by their deployment policy.

</details>


### [69] [Halt Fast! Early Stopping for Certified Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27694)
*Andrew C. Cullen,Paul Montague,Benjamin I. P. Rubinstein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于元学习的随机平滑框架，通过自适应资源分配实现了20倍样本复杂度降低，同时保持严格的鲁棒性认证。


<details>
